# CELPIP Listening — Standalone Practice Sets (Volume 1)

## Practice 1: Laptop Performance Troubleshooting | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a customer and a computer repair technician. The customer is experiencing issues with their laptop and is seeking advice on how to fix it.

### Audio Transcript
A: Welcome to Tech Solutions. How can I help you today?

B: Hi, my laptop's been acting up. It's really slow and keeps freezing.

A: I see. How long has this been happening?

B: For about a week now. It's getting worse.

A: Have you installed any new software recently?

B: No, nothing new.

A: Okay. Let's try a few things. First, restart your laptop and see if that helps.

B: I've done that multiple times, but it didn't work.

A: Alright. Let's check for malware. Do you have an antivirus program?

B: Yes, but I'm not sure if it's up to date.

A: Update it and run a full scan. If that doesn't work, we might need to check your hard drive.

B: How long will that take?

A: The scan could take a few hours. If issues persist, bring it in for a thorough check-up.

B: Okay, I'll try that. Thanks for your help.

**Q1:** What is the main issue the customer is experiencing?
- 1. The laptop won't turn on
- 2. The laptop is running slowly and freezing
- 3. The screen is flickering
- 4. The keyboard isn't working
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The laptop won't turn on: The customer doesn't mention any issues with turning on the laptop.
✅ 2. The laptop is running slowly and freezing: The customer explicitly states 'my laptop's been acting up. It's really slow and keeps freezing.'
❌ 3. The screen is flickering: There's no mention of screen flickering issues.
❌ 4. The keyboard isn't working: There's no mention of keyboard problems.

**Q2:** What has the customer already attempted to resolve the issue?
- 1. Updated the antivirus software
- 2. Checked the hard drive
- 3. Restarted the laptop
- 4. Installed new software
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Updated the antivirus software: The customer mentions they're not sure if their antivirus is up to date, indicating they haven't updated it.
❌ 2. Checked the hard drive: The customer has not mentioned checking the hard drive.
✅ 3. Restarted the laptop: The customer states 'I've done that multiple times, but it didn't work' when the technician suggests restarting the laptop.
❌ 4. Installed new software: The customer specifically says 'No, nothing new' when asked if they installed any new software.

**Q3:** What is suggested as the first troubleshooting step?
- 1. Running an antivirus scan
- 2. Checking the hard drive
- 3. Installing new software
- 4. Updating the antivirus program
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Running an antivirus scan: This is suggested after updating the antivirus program.
❌ 2. Checking the hard drive: This is mentioned as a later step if other solutions don't work.
❌ 3. Installing new software: Installing new software is not suggested as a solution.
✅ 4. Updating the antivirus program: The technician says 'Update it and run a full scan' as the first specific action after learning the restart didn't work.

**Q4:** How long has the customer been experiencing this problem?
- 1. A few hours
- 2. A few days
- 3. About a week
- 4. Several months
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A few hours: The customer did not mention experiencing the problem for only a few hours.
❌ 2. A few days: The customer did not specify 'a few days' as the timeframe.
✅ 3. About a week: The customer explicitly states 'For about a week now. It's getting worse.'
❌ 4. Several months: The customer did not mention experiencing the problem for several months.

**Q5:** What is implied about the customer's computer maintenance habits?
- 1. They regularly update their software
- 2. They don't maintain their computer well
- 3. They frequently install new programs
- 4. They run daily virus scans
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They regularly update their software: There's no evidence of regular updates in the conversation.
✅ 2. They don't maintain their computer well: This is implied when the customer says 'Yes, but I'm not sure if it's up to date' regarding their antivirus program, suggesting neglect of basic maintenance.
❌ 3. They frequently install new programs: The customer specifically states they haven't installed anything new recently.
❌ 4. They run daily virus scans: There's no mention of the customer running regular virus scans.

**Q6:** What is the tech support's final recommendation if initial solutions don't work?
- 1. Buy a new laptop
- 2. Install new software
- 3. Bring the laptop in for inspection
- 4. Reset the laptop to factory settings
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Buy a new laptop: The technician never suggests buying a new laptop.
❌ 2. Install new software: Installing new software is not mentioned as a solution.
✅ 3. Bring the laptop in for inspection: The technician says 'If issues persist, bring it in for a thorough check-up.'
❌ 4. Reset the laptop to factory settings: A factory reset is not mentioned as a solution.

## Practice 2: Gym Membership Cancellation | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a customer and a gym representative. The customer wants to cancel their gym membership.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why is the caller contacting Fitness Plus?
- 1. To upgrade their membership
- 2. To request a refund
- 3. To cancel their membership
- 4. To report equipment issues
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To upgrade their membership: The caller never mentions wanting to upgrade their membership.
❌ 2. To request a refund: The caller doesn't ask for any money back.
✅ 3. To cancel their membership: The caller clearly states 'I'd like to cancel my gym membership, please.'
❌ 4. To report equipment issues: No equipment problems are mentioned in the conversation.

**Q2:** What solution does the representative initially propose?
- 1. A temporary membership freeze
- 2. A membership transfer to a partner gym
- 3. A discounted rate
- 4. A partial refund
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A temporary membership freeze: The representative doesn't suggest freezing the membership.
✅ 2. A membership transfer to a partner gym: The representative asks 'Have you considered transferring your membership to one of our partner gyms in your new city?'
❌ 3. A discounted rate: No discount or reduced rate is offered.
❌ 4. A partial refund: No refund option is mentioned by the representative.

**Q3:** What is revealed about Fitness Plus?
- 1. They have strict cancellation policies
- 2. They only operate in select cities
- 3. They have a network of partner facilities
- 4. They require annual commitments
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They have strict cancellation policies: While there's a 30-day notice, this doesn't indicate the policies are particularly strict.
❌ 2. They only operate in select cities: The conversation doesn't reveal information about their own gym locations.
✅ 3. They have a network of partner facilities: The representative mentions 'We have partner gyms in several cities' showing they work with other gyms.
❌ 4. They require annual commitments: No information about membership commitment length is mentioned.

**Q4:** What is the outcome of the caller's request?
- 1. Immediate cancellation
- 2. Cancellation with 30 days notice
- 3. Membership transfer approved
- 4. Request denied
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Immediate cancellation: The representative explains there's a 30-day notice period, not immediate cancellation.
✅ 2. Cancellation with 30 days notice: The representative states 'Please note there's a 30-day notice period' and confirms the membership will be active for 30 more days.
❌ 3. Membership transfer approved: The transfer option was not available since there's no partner gym in Springfield.
❌ 4. Request denied: The cancellation request was approved, not denied.

**Q5:** How does the caller respond to the transfer option?
- 1. With enthusiasm
- 2. With skepticism
- 3. With initial interest
- 4. With immediate rejection
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. With enthusiasm: The caller doesn't show strong excitement about the transfer option.
❌ 2. With skepticism: The caller doesn't express doubt or distrust about the option.
✅ 3. With initial interest: The caller says 'That sounds interesting' showing some interest in the transfer option.
❌ 4. With immediate rejection: The caller doesn't reject the option right away, instead showing interest.

**Q6:** What can be inferred about the gym's cancellation policy?
- 1. It is negotiable
- 2. It is strictly enforced
- 3. It varies by location
- 4. It depends on membership type
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It is negotiable: The representative doesn't offer to negotiate the 30-day notice period.
✅ 2. It is strictly enforced: The representative clearly states the 30-day notice requirement without offering alternatives, indicating strict enforcement.
❌ 3. It varies by location: No information suggests the policy changes based on location.
❌ 4. It depends on membership type: The conversation doesn't mention different policies for different membership types.

## Practice 3: Connecting Flight Rescheduling | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a passenger and an airline customer service representative. The passenger is seeking assistance due to a delayed flight.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why is the customer calling Skyline Airlines?
- 1. To request a refund
- 2. To report lost luggage
- 3. To discuss a flight delay
- 4. To book a new flight
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To request a refund: The customer never mentions wanting a refund in the conversation.
❌ 2. To report lost luggage: There is no mention of lost luggage in the conversation.
✅ 3. To discuss a flight delay: The customer clearly states "I'm at the airport, and my flight has been delayed for several hours. I'm worried about missing my connecting flight."
❌ 4. To book a new flight: The customer is not trying to book a new flight, but rather dealing with an existing booking that has been delayed.

**Q2:** What solution does the airline representative initially offer?
- 1. A full refund
- 2. Hotel accommodation
- 3. An alternative connecting flight
- 4. Priority boarding
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A full refund: The representative never offers a refund.
❌ 2. Hotel accommodation: No hotel accommodation is mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 3. An alternative connecting flight: The representative says "There's a later connecting flight I can book you on, which would still get you to your final destination today."
❌ 4. Priority boarding: Priority boarding is not mentioned as a solution in the conversation.

**Q3:** What compensation does the airline provide?
- 1. Cash compensation
- 2. Free future flight
- 3. Upgrade to business class
- 4. Meal vouchers
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Cash compensation: No cash compensation is mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 2. Free future flight: The airline does not offer a free future flight.
❌ 3. Upgrade to business class: No upgrade is mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 4. Meal vouchers: The representative states "We'll also provide meal vouchers for your extended wait."

**Q4:** What caused the flight delay?
- 1. Weather conditions
- 2. Technical problems
- 3. Air traffic control
- 4. Crew shortage
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Weather conditions: Weather is not mentioned as the cause of the delay.
✅ 2. Technical problems: The representative specifically states "I see your flight has been delayed due to technical issues."
❌ 3. Air traffic control: Air traffic control is not mentioned as a cause.
❌ 4. Crew shortage: There is no mention of crew shortage in the conversation.

**Q5:** How does the customer feel about the solution offered?
- 1. Frustrated
- 2. Relieved
- 3. Skeptical
- 4. Indifferent
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Frustrated: The customer does not express frustration with the solution.
✅ 2. Relieved: The customer explicitly says "That's a relief" when told about the rebooking option.
❌ 3. Skeptical: The customer shows no signs of skepticism in the conversation.
❌ 4. Indifferent: The customer is clearly engaged and shows positive emotion, not indifference.

**Q6:** What must the customer do to receive the compensation?
- 1. Fill out a form online
- 2. Visit the customer service desk
- 3. Email customer support
- 4. Call back later
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Fill out a form online: No online form is mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. Visit the customer service desk: The representative clearly states "You can collect them [the meal vouchers] at the customer service desk."
❌ 3. Email customer support: Emailing is not mentioned as a way to receive compensation.
❌ 4. Call back later: The customer is not instructed to call back later.

## Practice 4: Restaurant Reservation Mix-Up | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a customer and a restaurant manager. The customer is calling about a problem with their reservation.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** What is the main issue discussed in the conversation?
- 1. A delayed food order
- 2. A pricing dispute
- 3. A booking error
- 4. A table location complaint
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A delayed food order: The conversation is about a reservation problem, not food delays.
❌ 2. A pricing dispute: No discussion about prices or billing issues occurred.
✅ 3. A booking error: The customer's reservation was accidentally entered for tomorrow night instead of tonight.
❌ 4. A table location complaint: The customer didn't complain about where their table was located.

**Q2:** When was the customer's reservation actually booked for?
- 1. Yesterday evening
- 2. The following night
- 3. Later that evening
- 4. Next week
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Yesterday evening: The manager did not mention yesterday as the booking date.
✅ 2. The following night: The manager said 'your reservation was accidentally entered for tomorrow night instead of tonight.'
❌ 3. Later that evening: The reservation was not for later the same night.
❌ 4. Next week: The manager specifically mentioned tomorrow, not next week.

**Q3:** What solution is initially offered to the customer?
- 1. A different restaurant recommendation
- 2. A full refund
- 3. A table after a short wait
- 4. A discount on the meal
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A different restaurant recommendation: The manager did not suggest going elsewhere.
❌ 2. A full refund: No refund was offered since no payment had been made yet.
✅ 3. A table after a short wait: The manager said 'We have a table that will be available in about 20 minutes.'
❌ 4. A discount on the meal: The initial solution was about seating, not pricing.

**Q4:** How does the restaurant compensate for the error?
- 1. A free meal
- 2. A complimentary appetizer and dessert
- 3. A future discount
- 4. A priority seating arrangement
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A free meal: The manager did not offer a completely free meal, only specific items.
❌ 2. A complimentary appetizer and dessert: The manager specifically said 'I'd be happy to offer you a complimentary appetizer and dessert for your party.'
✅ 3. A future discount: No discount for future visits was mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. A priority seating arrangement: While they offered a table in 20 minutes, this wasn't described as priority seating.

**Q5:** What can be inferred about the customer's attitude?
- 1. Extremely angry
- 2. Completely satisfied
- 3. Initially upset but willing to compromise
- 4. Indifferent to the situation
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Extremely angry: The customer expressed frustration but remained polite and reasonable.
❌ 2. Completely satisfied: The customer was clearly frustrated about the mistake initially.
✅ 3. Initially upset but willing to compromise: The customer said 'That's frustrating' but agreed to wait and accepted the compensation.
❌ 4. Indifferent to the situation: The customer clearly cared about the situation and expressed frustration.

**Q6:** How many people were in the customer's party?
- 1. Two
- 2. Three
- 3. Four
- 4. Five
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Two: The customer clearly stated a different number.
❌ 2. Three: This is not the number mentioned by the customer.
✅ 3. Four: The customer specifically said 'It's for tonight at 7 PM, for a party of four.'
❌ 4. Five: The customer did not mention five people in their party.

## Practice 5: Rental Car Confirmation | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a customer and a car rental company representative. The customer is having trouble with their online booking.

### Audio Transcript
$4c

**Q1:** Why is the customer calling EasyDrive Car Rentals?
- 1. To cancel a reservation
- 2. To make a new booking
- 3. To report a missing confirmation email
- 4. To complain about poor service
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To cancel a reservation: The customer never mentions wanting to cancel their booking.
❌ 2. To make a new booking: The customer already made a reservation yesterday.
✅ 3. To report a missing confirmation email: The customer explicitly states "I made a reservation yesterday, but I can't find the confirmation email."
❌ 4. To complain about poor service: The customer is seeking help with a specific issue, not complaining about service quality.

**Q2:** What caused the customer's initial problem?
- 1. The customer entered wrong email address
- 2. A technical problem with the system
- 3. The reservation wasn't properly completed
- 4. The email went to spam folder
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The customer entered wrong email address: This is not mentioned as the cause.
✅ 2. A technical problem with the system: The representative explains "there was a system glitch that prevented the confirmation email from being sent."
❌ 3. The reservation wasn't properly completed: The reservation was completed, but the confirmation email wasn't sent due to a system glitch.
❌ 4. The email went to spam folder: This possibility is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q3:** What change does the customer request?
- 1. The rental location
- 2. The pickup date
- 3. The rental duration
- 4. The pickup time
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The rental location: The customer doesn't request to change the location.
❌ 2. The pickup date: The customer doesn't ask to change the date.
❌ 3. The rental duration: There's no mention of changing how long they'll rent the car.
✅ 4. The pickup time: The customer specifically asks "Is it possible to move it from 2 PM to 4 PM?"

**Q4:** What is the original pickup time for the reservation?
- 1. 4 PM
- 2. 2 PM
- 3. The time is not mentioned
- 4. 12 PM
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 4 PM: This is the new time requested by the customer, not the original time.
✅ 2. 2 PM: The customer asks to change the time "from 2 PM to 4 PM," indicating 2 PM was the original time.
❌ 3. The time is not mentioned: The original time (2 PM) is clearly mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. 12 PM: This time is not mentioned anywhere in the conversation.

**Q5:** What will happen after the call?
- 1. The customer needs to call back
- 2. The customer will receive a new email
- 3. The customer must visit the office
- 4. The reservation will be cancelled
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The customer needs to call back: There's no indication the customer needs to call again.
✅ 2. The customer will receive a new email: The representative states "You'll receive a new confirmation email shortly" after updating the reservation.
❌ 3. The customer must visit the office: This is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. The reservation will be cancelled: The reservation is being modified, not cancelled.

**Q6:** Where is the customer planning to pick up the car?
- 1. At the downtown location
- 2. At the airport
- 3. At the customer's home
- 4. At a hotel
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. At the downtown location: This location is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. At the airport: The customer states the reservation is "for next Friday at the airport."
❌ 3. At the customer's home: This is not mentioned as a pickup location.
❌ 4. At a hotel: There is no mention of a hotel in the conversation.

## Practice 6: Smart Thermostat Troubleshooting | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a customer and a smart home device support representative. The customer is experiencing issues with their smart thermostat.

### Audio Transcript
A: Thank you for contacting Smart Home Support. How may I assist you today?

B: Hi, I'm having trouble with my smart thermostat. It's not responding to my app commands.

A: I understand. Let's troubleshoot this together. Is the thermostat powered on?

B: Yes, it's on, but the screen is frozen.

A: Okay. Let's try a quick reset. Can you remove the thermostat from its base for 30 seconds?

B: Alright, I've done that. Now what?

A: Please reattach it. Does the display come back on?

B: Yes, it's working now, but still not connecting to my app.

A: Let's check your Wi-Fi. Is your home network working properly?

B: Yes, all my other devices are connected.

A: I see. I'll need to reset your device's network settings. This might take a few minutes.

B: Okay, I'll wait.

A: Great, it's done. Can you try connecting through your app now?

B: It's working! Thank you so much for your help.

A: You're welcome. Is there anything else I can assist you with?

**Q1:** What is the main issue the customer is experiencing?
- 1. The thermostat is not turning on
- 2. The app is not installed correctly
- 3. The thermostat is not responding to app commands
- 4. The Wi-Fi network is down
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The thermostat is not turning on: The customer stated the thermostat was powered on but frozen.
❌ 2. The app is not installed correctly: There's no mention of app installation issues.
✅ 3. The thermostat is not responding to app commands: The customer explicitly stated 'I'm having trouble with my smart thermostat. It's not responding to my app commands.'
❌ 4. The Wi-Fi network is down: The customer confirmed their Wi-Fi was working properly.

**Q2:** What was the first troubleshooting step suggested?
- 1. Checking the Wi-Fi connection
- 2. Resetting network settings
- 3. Installing the app again
- 4. Removing the thermostat from its base
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Checking the Wi-Fi connection: This was suggested later in the conversation, not first.
❌ 2. Resetting network settings: This was the final solution, not the first step.
❌ 3. Installing the app again: This was never suggested in the conversation.
✅ 4. Removing the thermostat from its base: The representative first suggested 'Let's try a quick reset. Can you remove the thermostat from its base for 30 seconds?'

**Q3:** What was discovered about the customer's Wi-Fi network?
- 1. It was working properly
- 2. It needed to be reset
- 3. It was disconnected
- 4. It had a weak signal
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. It was working properly: The customer confirmed 'Yes, all my other devices are connected' when asked if their home network was working properly.
❌ 2. It needed to be reset: The conversation doesn't mention the Wi-Fi network needing to be reset, only the device's network settings.
❌ 3. It was disconnected: The customer confirmed their network was working and other devices were connected.
❌ 4. It had a weak signal: There's no mention of signal strength in the conversation.

**Q4:** What was the final solution to the problem?
- 1. Replacing the thermostat
- 2. Resetting the device's network settings
- 3. Updating the app
- 4. Restarting the Wi-Fi router
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Replacing the thermostat: There was no mention of replacing the thermostat.
✅ 2. Resetting the device's network settings: The representative said 'I'll need to reset your device's network settings' which resolved the issue.
❌ 3. Updating the app: There was no mention of updating the app.
❌ 4. Restarting the Wi-Fi router: There was no mention of restarting the router.

**Q5:** What happened when the customer first removed and reattached the thermostat?
- 1. Nothing changed
- 2. The problem was completely resolved
- 3. The display worked but app connection failed
- 4. The thermostat stopped working entirely
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Nothing changed: This is incorrect as the display did start working again.
❌ 2. The problem was completely resolved: The problem wasn't completely resolved as the app connection still failed.
✅ 3. The display worked but app connection failed: After reattaching the thermostat, the customer said 'Yes, it's working now, but still not connecting to my app.'
❌ 4. The thermostat stopped working entirely: The thermostat actually started working again, at least partially.

**Q6:** How did the support representative approach the problem?
- 1. By immediately suggesting a replacement
- 2. By following a step-by-step process
- 3. By transferring to another department
- 4. By scheduling a technician visit
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. By immediately suggesting a replacement: The representative never suggested replacing the thermostat.
✅ 2. By following a step-by-step process: The representative methodically worked through different troubleshooting steps - checking power, resetting the thermostat, checking Wi-Fi, and finally resetting network settings.
❌ 3. By transferring to another department: No transfer to another department was mentioned.
❌ 4. By scheduling a technician visit: No technician visit was scheduled or mentioned.

## Practice 7: Sweater Return Inquiry | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a customer and a customer service representative from an online store. The customer is trying to return an item they purchased.

### Audio Transcript
A: Thank you for contacting OnlineShop customer service. How may I assist you today?

B: Hi, I need to return an item I bought last week.

A: I'd be happy to help with that. Can you provide your order number?

B: Sure, it's OD987654.

A: Thank you. I see you purchased a blue sweater. What's the reason for the return?

B: It's too small. I'd like to exchange it for a larger size.

A: I understand. Unfortunately, we're out of stock in larger sizes. Would you prefer a refund?

B: Oh, that's disappointing. Yes, a refund would be fine then.

A: I apologize for the inconvenience. I'll process the refund now. Please print the return label I'm emailing you and send the item back within 14 days.

B: Okay, I'll do that. How long will the refund take?

A: Once we receive the item, the refund will be processed within 3-5 business days.

B: Alright, thank you for your help.

A: You're welcome. Is there anything else I can assist you with today?

B: No, that's all. Thank you.

**Q1:** What is the main purpose of this conversation?
- 1. To track a missing package
- 2. To process a return
- 3. To place a new order
- 4. To report damaged goods
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To track a missing package: The conversation is not about tracking a package.
✅ 2. To process a return: The customer explicitly states "I need to return an item I bought last week" at the beginning of the conversation.
❌ 3. To place a new order: The customer is not placing a new order in this conversation.
❌ 4. To report damaged goods: The customer wants to return the item because it's too small, not because it's damaged.

**Q2:** Why can't the customer exchange the item?
- 1. The return period has expired
- 2. The item is damaged
- 3. Larger sizes are unavailable
- 4. Exchanges are not allowed
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The return period has expired: There is no mention of an expired return period.
❌ 2. The item is damaged: The item is not damaged; it's too small.
✅ 3. Larger sizes are unavailable: The representative specifically states "Unfortunately, we're out of stock in larger sizes."
❌ 4. Exchanges are not allowed: The representative does not say exchanges aren't allowed; they just don't have the larger size in stock.

**Q3:** What must the customer do to receive the refund?
- 1. Visit a local store
- 2. Pay for return shipping
- 3. Call customer service again
- 4. Return the item within 14 days
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Visit a local store: There is no mention of visiting a store in the conversation.
❌ 2. Pay for return shipping: There is no mention of the customer needing to pay for return shipping.
❌ 3. Call customer service again: The customer is not instructed to call customer service again.
✅ 4. Return the item within 14 days: The representative instructs the customer to "send the item back within 14 days" to receive the refund.

**Q4:** How does the customer initially feel about the solution offered?
- 1. Satisfied
- 2. Angry
- 3. Disappointed
- 4. Confused
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Satisfied: The customer does not express satisfaction with the initial solution.
❌ 2. Angry: The customer does not express anger in the conversation.
✅ 3. Disappointed: When told that larger sizes are out of stock, the customer says "Oh, that's disappointing" showing their disappointment with the situation.
❌ 4. Confused: The customer does not express confusion during the conversation.

**Q5:** What information does the customer provide to start the return process?
- 1. Email address
- 2. Order number
- 3. Phone number
- 4. Shipping address
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Email address: The customer does not provide an email address during the conversation.
✅ 2. Order number: The customer provides the order number OD987654 when the representative asks for it to begin the return process.
❌ 3. Phone number: The customer does not provide a phone number during the conversation.
❌ 4. Shipping address: The customer does not provide a shipping address during the conversation.

**Q6:** When will the customer receive their refund?
- 1. Immediately
- 2. Within 14 days
- 3. 3-5 days after item receipt
- 4. At the end of the month
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Immediately: The refund is not processed immediately.
❌ 2. Within 14 days: 14 days is the timeframe for returning the item, not receiving the refund.
✅ 3. 3-5 days after item receipt: The representative clearly states "Once we receive the item, the refund will be processed within 3-5 business days."
❌ 4. At the end of the month: There is no mention of the end of the month in the conversation.

## Practice 9: Printer Driver Troubleshooting | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a customer and a technical support representative. The customer is experiencing issues with their printer and is seeking assistance.

### Audio Transcript
A: Thank you for contacting Tech Support. How may I assist you today?

B: Hi, I'm having trouble with my printer. It's not printing anything.

A: I understand. Let's troubleshoot this together. Is the printer turned on and connected properly?

B: Yes, it's on and connected to my computer via USB.

A: Okay. Are there any error messages on the printer's display?

B: No, there's nothing showing up.

A: Let's check the ink levels. Can you open the printer cover and tell me if any cartridges appear empty?

B: They all look fine to me.

A: Alright. Let's try a test print. Can you go to your computer's settings and find the printers section?

B: Yes, I'm there.

A: Great. Select your printer and click 'Print Test Page'.

B: It's not doing anything.

A: I see. It seems we may need to reinstall the printer drivers. I can guide you through that process if you'd like.

B: Yes, please. That would be helpful.

A: Certainly. I'll walk you through it step by step.

**Q1:** What is the main issue the customer is experiencing?
- 1. The printer is making strange noises
- 2. The printer won't connect to Wi-Fi
- 3. The printer is not producing any output
- 4. The printer is showing error messages
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The printer is making strange noises: The customer never mentions any noises from the printer.
❌ 2. The printer won't connect to Wi-Fi: Wi-Fi connection is not mentioned; the customer states it's connected via USB.
✅ 3. The printer is not producing any output: The customer clearly states "It's not printing anything" at the beginning of the conversation.
❌ 4. The printer is showing error messages: The customer specifically says there are no error messages showing up.

**Q2:** What has the customer already verified?
- 1. The ink cartridge colors
- 2. The power and connection status
- 3. The printer software version
- 4. The paper quality settings
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The ink cartridge colors: The customer doesn't mention checking ink cartridge colors before calling.
✅ 2. The power and connection status: The customer confirms "Yes, it's on and connected to my computer via USB" when asked by the agent.
❌ 3. The printer software version: Software version is never mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. The paper quality settings: Paper quality settings are not discussed at any point.

**Q3:** What does the support agent suggest checking first?
- 1. The printer drivers
- 2. The USB connection
- 3. The error messages
- 4. The network settings
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The printer drivers: The agent only suggests checking drivers later in the conversation, not first.
✅ 2. The USB connection: The agent's first troubleshooting question is "Is the printer turned on and connected properly?" which refers to the power and connection status.
❌ 3. The error messages: The agent checks for error messages second, after confirming the printer is on and connected.
❌ 4. The network settings: Network settings are never mentioned in the conversation.

**Q4:** What step did the agent recommend before suggesting driver reinstallation?
- 1. Checking ink levels
- 2. Running a test print
- 3. Restarting the printer
- 4. Updating the software
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Checking ink levels: While the agent did suggest checking ink levels, this wasn't the step immediately before suggesting driver reinstallation.
✅ 2. Running a test print: The agent's last troubleshooting step before suggesting driver reinstallation was "Let's try a test print. Can you go to your computer's settings and find the printers section?"
❌ 3. Restarting the printer: The agent never suggests restarting the printer in the conversation.
❌ 4. Updating the software: Software updates are not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q5:** What appears to be the likely solution to the problem?
- 1. Replacing the ink cartridges
- 2. Purchasing a new printer
- 3. Reinstalling the printer drivers
- 4. Changing the USB port
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Replacing the ink cartridges: The customer confirms the ink cartridges look fine, so this isn't presented as the solution.
❌ 2. Purchasing a new printer: The agent never suggests buying a new printer.
✅ 3. Reinstalling the printer drivers: After exhausting other options, the agent says "It seems we may need to reinstall the printer drivers" and offers to guide the customer through this process.
❌ 4. Changing the USB port: Changing the USB port is never suggested in the conversation.

**Q6:** How does the support agent approach the problem-solving process?
- 1. By immediately suggesting driver reinstallation
- 2. By asking the customer to buy new equipment
- 3. By systematic elimination of possible causes
- 4. By transferring the call to another department
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. By immediately suggesting driver reinstallation: The agent doesn't immediately suggest reinstalling drivers; this is only suggested after trying several other troubleshooting steps.
❌ 2. By asking the customer to buy new equipment: The agent never suggests purchasing new equipment at any point in the conversation.
✅ 3. By systematic elimination of possible causes: The agent follows a methodical approach, checking power/connection, error messages, ink levels, and test printing before suggesting driver reinstallation.
❌ 4. By transferring the call to another department: The agent handles the entire call without transferring it to anyone else.

## Practice 10: Library Book Return | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a library patron and a librarian. The patron is calling about an issue with returning a book.

### Audio Transcript
A: Hello, City Library. How may I assist you today?

B: Hi, I'm calling about a book I borrowed. I returned it last week, but it's still showing as checked out on my account.

A: I see. Let me look into that for you. Can you provide the title of the book?

B: It's 'The Art of Gardening'.

A: Thank you. I'm checking our system now. It appears the book hasn't been scanned as returned. Did you use the drop box or return it at the counter?

B: I used the drop box as it was after hours.

A: I understand. Sometimes books can get stuck in the drop box. I'll have someone check it right away. In the meantime, I've marked the book as returned in our system to avoid any late fees.

B: That's a relief. Thank you for your help.

A: You're welcome. Is there anything else I can assist you with today?

B: No, that's all. Thanks again.

A: My pleasure. Have a great day!

**Q1:** Why is the person calling the library?
- 1. To renew a book loan
- 2. To report a missing book
- 3. To dispute a record of a book return
- 4. To ask about library hours
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To renew a book loan: The caller doesn't mention wanting to renew anything.
❌ 2. To report a missing book: The book isn't missing - it was returned but not recorded properly.
✅ 3. To dispute a record of a book return: The caller states "I returned it last week, but it's still showing as checked out on my account," which is disputing the return record.
❌ 4. To ask about library hours: There is no mention of library hours in the conversation.

**Q2:** What appears to be the issue with the book return?
- 1. The book was damaged
- 2. The book wasn't properly scanned
- 3. The caller forgot to return it
- 4. The system was offline
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The book was damaged: There's no mention of any damage to the book.
✅ 2. The book wasn't properly scanned: The librarian states "It appears the book hasn't been scanned as returned" which directly identifies this as the issue.
❌ 3. The caller forgot to return it: The caller clearly states they returned the book last week.
❌ 4. The system was offline: There's no mention of the system being offline in the conversation.

**Q3:** How did the caller return the book?
- 1. Through interlibrary loan
- 2. At the circulation desk
- 3. Through the book drop
- 4. By mail
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Through interlibrary loan: The conversation doesn't mention interlibrary loan at all.
❌ 2. At the circulation desk: The caller specifically states they did not return it at the counter.
✅ 3. Through the book drop: The caller clearly states "I used the drop box as it was after hours."
❌ 4. By mail: There is no mention of mailing the book in the conversation.

**Q4:** What immediate action did the library staff take?
- 1. Asked the caller to bring proof of return
- 2. Charged a late fee
- 3. Updated the system to show the book as returned
- 4. Requested the book be returned again
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Asked the caller to bring proof of return: The librarian did not ask for proof of return.
❌ 2. Charged a late fee: The librarian specifically took action to avoid late fees, not charge them.
✅ 3. Updated the system to show the book as returned: The librarian states "I've marked the book as returned in our system to avoid any late fees."
❌ 4. Requested the book be returned again: The librarian did not ask for the book to be returned again.

**Q5:** What can be inferred about the library's drop box?
- 1. It is only available during business hours
- 2. It sometimes has technical issues
- 3. It requires a library card to use
- 4. It is regularly monitored
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It is only available during business hours: The conversation indicates the opposite - the caller used it "after hours."
✅ 2. It sometimes has technical issues: The librarian says "Sometimes books can get stuck in the drop box," suggesting there are occasional problems with it.
❌ 3. It requires a library card to use: There is no mention of needing a library card to use the drop box.
❌ 4. It is regularly monitored: If it were regularly monitored, the book would likely have been found already. The librarian says "I'll have someone check it right away," suggesting it isn't constantly monitored.

**Q6:** What was the caller's reaction to the solution?
- 1. Frustrated
- 2. Grateful
- 3. Confused
- 4. Indifferent
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Frustrated: The caller shows no signs of frustration in their response.
✅ 2. Grateful: The caller says "That's a relief. Thank you for your help" and "Thanks again," clearly showing gratitude.
❌ 3. Confused: There's no indication the caller is confused by the solution.
❌ 4. Indifferent: The caller's response shows they care about the outcome, not indifference.

❌ 2. Complete rejection: The guest did not completely reject the offer.

❌ 4. Anger at the suggestion: The guest shows no signs of anger in the conversation.

A: Perfect. Let's update the firmware. Make sure your tracker is charged and close to your phone during the update.

B: Alright, I've started the update. How long will it take?

B: Okay, I'll do that. Thanks for your help.

**Q1:** What is the main issue the caller is experiencing?

- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Replacing the battery: This was not mentioned in the conversation.

❌ 3. Reinstalling the app: This was not mentioned in the conversation.

- 2. The tracker was damaged

- 4. The app was corrupted

- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The phone was incompatible: This was not discovered during the conversation.

## Practice 14: Delayed Package Inquiry | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a customer and a delivery company representative. The customer is inquiring about a package that hasn't arrived on time.

### Audio Transcript
A: Thank you for calling Express Delivery. How may I assist you today?

B: Hi, I'm calling about a package that was supposed to arrive yesterday, but it hasn't shown up.

A: I apologize for the delay. Can you provide your tracking number?

B: Sure, it's ED789456123.

A: Thank you. Let me check that for you. It appears your package is currently at our local distribution center. There was a delay due to severe weather conditions yesterday.

B: I see. When can I expect it to be delivered?

A: Based on the current status, it should be delivered by the end of today. Would you like me to arrange for a specific delivery time?

B: Yes, that would be helpful. Can it be delivered after 5 PM?

A: Certainly. I've updated the delivery instructions for evening delivery between 5 PM and 8 PM. Is there anything else I can help you with?

B: No, that's all. Thank you for your assistance.

A: You're welcome. Have a great day!

**Q1:** What is the main reason for the customer's call?
- 1. To schedule a package pickup
- 2. To report a missing package
- 3. To inquire about a delayed delivery
- 4. To cancel a delivery
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To schedule a package pickup: The customer is not calling to schedule a pickup, but about a package that hasn't arrived.
❌ 2. To report a missing package: While the package hasn't arrived, the customer is specifically inquiring about a delay rather than reporting it as missing.
✅ 3. To inquire about a delayed delivery: The customer states "I'm calling about a package that was supposed to arrive yesterday, but it hasn't shown up," which is clearly inquiring about a delayed delivery.
❌ 4. To cancel a delivery: The customer never mentions wanting to cancel the delivery.

**Q2:** What caused the delivery delay?
- 1. Staff shortage
- 2. Weather conditions
- 3. Wrong address
- 4. Technical issues
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Staff shortage: This reason is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. Weather conditions: The representative specifically states "There was a delay due to severe weather conditions yesterday."
❌ 3. Wrong address: There is no mention of an address issue in the conversation.
❌ 4. Technical issues: Technical problems are not mentioned as a cause for the delay.

**Q3:** Where is the package currently located?
- 1. At the customer's address
- 2. In transit
- 3. At the local distribution center
- 4. At the main warehouse
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. At the customer's address: The package has not been delivered yet.
❌ 2. In transit: The package is not described as being in transit.
✅ 3. At the local distribution center: The representative clearly states "It appears your package is currently at our local distribution center."
❌ 4. At the main warehouse: A main warehouse is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q4:** What time window was arranged for delivery?
- 1. 9 AM to 12 PM
- 2. 1 PM to 4 PM
- 3. 3 PM to 6 PM
- 4. 5 PM to 8 PM
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 9 AM to 12 PM: This time window was not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 2. 1 PM to 4 PM: This time window was not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 3. 3 PM to 6 PM: This time window was not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 4. 5 PM to 8 PM: The representative specifically states "I've updated the delivery instructions for evening delivery between 5 PM and 8 PM."

**Q5:** What can be inferred about the customer?
- 1. They are at home during the day
- 2. They are likely working during regular hours
- 3. They frequently use this delivery service
- 4. They are dissatisfied with the service
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They are at home during the day: There's no indication of this in the conversation.
✅ 2. They are likely working during regular hours: This can be inferred because the customer specifically requests delivery "after 5 PM," suggesting they are not available during standard business hours.
❌ 3. They frequently use this delivery service: There's no information in the conversation to support this inference.
❌ 4. They are dissatisfied with the service: While the customer is inquiring about a delay, they remain polite and thank the representative at the end, showing no clear dissatisfaction.

**Q6:** What was the customer service representative's initial response?
- 1. Offered a refund
- 2. Provided an explanation
- 3. Requested tracking information
- 4. Suggested an alternative delivery date
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Offered a refund: No refund was offered in the initial response.
❌ 2. Provided an explanation: The representative first apologized and asked for tracking information before providing any explanation.
✅ 3. Requested tracking information: After apologizing, the representative's first action was "Can you provide your tracking number?"
❌ 4. Suggested an alternative delivery date: This was not part of the initial response.

## Practice 15: Surprise Birthday Party Budget | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two friends. They are discussing plans for a surprise birthday party.

### Audio Transcript
$4c

**Q1:** What is the main purpose of the conversation?
- 1. Planning a wedding reception
- 2. Organizing a birthday celebration
- 3. Arranging a community event
- 4. Discussing party arrangements
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Planning a wedding reception: The conversation is clearly about a birthday party, not a wedding.
✅ 2. Organizing a birthday celebration: Person A explicitly states 'We need to finalize the plans for the surprise party' and they mention 'the guest of honor', indicating it's a surprise birthday party.
❌ 3. Arranging a community event: While they're using a community center, it's for a private birthday party, not a community event.
❌ 4. Discussing party arrangements: This is too general - the specific purpose is organizing a birthday celebration.

**Q2:** Where will the event take place?
- 1. At a restaurant
- 2. In a public facility
- 3. At someone's home
- 4. In a private hall
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. At a restaurant: There's no mention of holding the event at a restaurant.
✅ 2. In a public facility: Person A says 'I've reserved the community center', which is a public facility available for community use.
❌ 3. At someone's home: The conversation clearly states they've booked a venue, not using someone's home.
❌ 4. In a private hall: A community center is a public facility, not a private hall.

**Q3:** What decision was made about the food?
- 1. Hiring professional services
- 2. Ordering from a restaurant
- 3. Combining self-prepared dishes with contributions
- 4. Having a buffet service
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Hiring professional services: Person B initially suggested hiring a catering service, but they decided against it.
❌ 2. Ordering from a restaurant: There's no mention of ordering food from a restaurant.
✅ 3. Combining self-prepared dishes with contributions: Person A suggested preparing food themselves, and Person B agreed they could make simple dishes and ask guests to bring something too.
❌ 4. Having a buffet service: There's no mention of a buffet service in the conversation.

**Q4:** How will music be provided at the event?
- 1. Through a DJ service
- 2. By recorded music
- 3. With live entertainment
- 4. By guest performances
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Through a DJ service: The conversation doesn't mention hiring a DJ service.
✅ 2. By recorded music: Person A suggested hiring a local band, but Person B suggested creating a playlist instead, which Person A agreed to. A playlist means recorded music.
❌ 3. With live entertainment: Person A initially suggested hiring a local band (live entertainment), but they decided against it in favor of a playlist.
❌ 4. By guest performances: There's no mention of guests performing at the party.

**Q5:** What is the main reason for choosing certain options over others?
- 1. Time constraints
- 2. Space limitations
- 3. Cost considerations
- 4. Guest preferences
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Time constraints: The conversation doesn't mention time being a limiting factor in their decisions.
❌ 2. Space limitations: There's no discussion about space being an issue.
✅ 3. Cost considerations: Throughout the conversation, they consistently choose cheaper options - the community center is 'within budget', they avoid expensive catering, choose a playlist over hiring a band to 'save money for decorations'.
❌ 4. Guest preferences: The decisions aren't based on what guests prefer, but on budget considerations.

## Practice 16: Tech Startup Job Offer | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two friends. One friend is considering a new job opportunity and seeking advice.

### Audio Transcript
A: Hey, how's it going? You sounded excited on the phone.

B: I am! I just got offered a new job at a tech startup.

A: That's great news! Congratulations! What's the position?

B: It's a senior developer role. The salary is higher than my current job, and they offer stock options.

A: Sounds promising. What's holding you back?

B: Well, it's a startup, so there's more risk. And the hours might be longer.

A: That's true. How does it compare to your current job?

B: My current job is stable, but there's not much room for growth. This new opportunity could be a big step up.

A: Have you considered the work-life balance?

B: That's my main concern. I'm worried about burnout.

A: Valid point. Maybe you could negotiate flexible hours?

B: That's a good idea. I'll bring that up when I talk to them next.

A: Whatever you decide, make sure it aligns with your long-term goals.

B: You're right. I'll think it over carefully before making a decision. Thanks for the advice!

**Q1:** What is the main topic of the conversation?
- 1. A promotion opportunity
- 2. A career change decision
- 3. A salary negotiation
- 4. A workplace conflict
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A promotion opportunity: This is about a new job at a different company, not a promotion.
✅ 2. A career change decision: The entire conversation is about Person B considering whether to take a new job offer and weighing the pros and cons.
❌ 3. A salary negotiation: They're not discussing negotiating salary terms.
❌ 4. A workplace conflict: No conflict is mentioned in the conversation.

**Q2:** What benefit does the new position offer?
- 1. Flexible working hours
- 2. Better health insurance
- 3. Company shares
- 4. Remote work options
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Flexible working hours: This is suggested as something to negotiate, not currently offered.
❌ 2. Better health insurance: Health benefits are not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 3. Company shares: Person B specifically mentions 'they offer stock options' as part of the new job package.
❌ 4. Remote work options: Remote work is never discussed in the conversation.

**Q3:** What is the main drawback of the current position?
- 1. Poor management
- 2. Limited advancement opportunities
- 3. Low salary
- 4. Difficult colleagues
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Poor management: The conversation doesn't mention any issues with management at the current job.
✅ 2. Limited advancement opportunities: Person B clearly states 'My current job is stable, but there's not much room for growth.'
❌ 3. Low salary: While the new job offers higher salary, this isn't mentioned as the main problem with the current position.
❌ 4. Difficult colleagues: No mention of colleague problems in the conversation.

**Q4:** What is the primary concern about the new job?
- 1. Company stability
- 2. Office location
- 3. Work schedule demands
- 4. Team dynamics
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Company stability: While risk is mentioned, the main personal concern is different.
❌ 2. Office location: Location is never discussed in the conversation.
✅ 3. Work schedule demands: Person B says 'That's my main concern. I'm worried about burnout' when discussing work-life balance and longer hours.
❌ 4. Team dynamics: Team relationships are not mentioned as a concern.

**Q5:** What suggestion is made about the new position?
- 1. Ask for a higher salary
- 2. Request adjustable hours
- 3. Demand more benefits
- 4. Seek a different role
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Ask for a higher salary: No suggestion is made about requesting more money.
✅ 2. Request adjustable hours: Person A suggests 'Maybe you could negotiate flexible hours?' to address the work-life balance concern.
❌ 3. Demand more benefits: Benefits negotiation is not suggested.
❌ 4. Seek a different role: The suggestion is about modifying the current offer, not finding a different position.

❌ 3. Construction advice: The neighbor doesn't provide any construction advice during the conversation.
✅ 4. Meals at their home: The neighbor specifically says "you're always welcome to come over for dinner" as an offer to provide relief from the renovation chaos.

- 4. Language classes

- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Scuba diving: This activity is not mentioned in the conversation.

❌ 3. Shopping tours: Shopping tours are not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. Language classes: Language classes are not mentioned in the conversation.

- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Lack of community spirit: While community building is mentioned as a benefit, it's not the main concern that led to starting the program.

❌ 3. Poor street lighting: Street lighting issues are not mentioned in the conversation.

- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Every evening: Person A clarifies that it's not every night.
❌ 2. Once a week: This frequency is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 3. Once or twice monthly: Person A states 'I think they're looking for volunteers to do maybe one or two shifts a month.'
❌ 4. Three times per week: This frequency is not mentioned and would be much more than what Person A described.

**Q3:** What expenses are involved in joining the program?

- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Monthly membership dues: The conversation doesn't mention any monthly dues.
❌ 2. Training course fees: No training courses or fees are mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 3. Uniform costs: There is no mention of uniforms or related costs.

- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Next Monday: The conversation doesn't mention Monday as the meeting day.

❌ 3. Next Wednesday: Wednesday is not mentioned as the meeting day in the conversation.
❌ 4. Next Thursday: Thursday is not mentioned as the meeting day in the conversation.

## Practice 27: Sustainable Clothing Movement | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation. A journalist is interviewing an expert about sustainable fashion trends.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** What is the main purpose of this discussion?
- 1. To promote specific clothing brands
- 2. To explain eco-friendly fashion concepts
- 3. To criticize the fashion industry
- 4. To discuss clothing prices
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To promote specific clothing brands: No specific brands are promoted in the discussion.
✅ 2. To explain eco-friendly fashion concepts: The entire interview focuses on explaining what sustainable fashion is, its trends, challenges, and future developments.
❌ 3. To criticize the fashion industry: While traditional fashion's issues are mentioned, the tone is informative rather than critical.
❌ 4. To discuss clothing prices: While cost is mentioned as a challenge, it's not the main focus of the discussion.

**Q2:** What distinguishes environmentally conscious clothing production?
- 1. Quick manufacturing processes
- 2. Low-cost materials
- 3. Ethical manufacturing standards
- 4. Trendy designs
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Quick manufacturing processes: The expert contrasts sustainable fashion with traditional fashion's "rapid production," suggesting sustainable fashion doesn't prioritize speed.
❌ 2. Low-cost materials: The expert mentions higher costs as a challenge for sustainable production.
✅ 3. Ethical manufacturing standards: The expert explicitly states that sustainable fashion "focuses on ethical practices, eco-friendly materials, and longevity of garments."
❌ 4. Trendy designs: Not mentioned as a distinguishing feature of sustainable fashion.

**Q3:** Which current development is mentioned in the discussion?
- 1. Online shopping platforms
- 2. Fashion show regulations
- 3. Clothing rental services
- 4. Designer collaborations
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Online shopping platforms: Not mentioned in the conversation as a current trend in sustainable fashion.
❌ 2. Fashion show regulations: Not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 3. Clothing rental services: The expert specifically mentions "a growing interest in rental and second-hand clothing" as one of the key trends in sustainable fashion.
❌ 4. Designer collaborations: Not mentioned in the conversation as a current trend.

**Q4:** What is suggested for environmentally conscious shoppers?
- 1. Buy trendy items frequently
- 2. Focus on durability
- 3. Ignore product pricing
- 4. Purchase seasonal collections
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Buy trendy items frequently: This contradicts the advice given, which emphasizes buying less.
✅ 2. Focus on durability: The expert advises consumers to "buy less and choosing quality over quantity" which directly relates to focusing on durability.
❌ 3. Ignore product pricing: Not mentioned; in fact, the expert acknowledges cost as a challenge for sustainable fashion.
❌ 4. Purchase seasonal collections: Not mentioned and contradicts the advice to buy less.

**Q5:** What obstacle is mentioned regarding eco-friendly clothing?
- 1. Limited style options
- 2. Production expenses
- 3. Shipping difficulties
- 4. Color limitations
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Limited style options: Not mentioned as a challenge in the discussion.
✅ 2. Production expenses: The expert specifically states "One major challenge is the higher cost of sustainable production, which can make these products less accessible."
❌ 3. Shipping difficulties: Not mentioned as a challenge in the discussion.
❌ 4. Color limitations: Not mentioned as a challenge in the discussion.

**Q6:** What future development is anticipated?
- 1. Decreased consumer interest
- 2. Lower production standards
- 3. Advanced material innovation
- 4. Reduced brand competition
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Decreased consumer interest: The opposite is implied; the expert anticipates more pressure from consumers on brands.
❌ 2. Lower production standards: The expert anticipates improved standards, not lower ones.
✅ 3. Advanced material innovation: The expert specifically states "We expect to see more technological innovations in fabric production and recycling."
❌ 4. Reduced brand competition: Not mentioned; the expert actually mentions increased pressure on major brands to adopt sustainable practices.

## Practice 28: Renewable Energy Transition | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation. A reporter is interviewing an energy expert about the future of renewable energy sources.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** What is the main purpose of this discussion?
- 1. To debate environmental policies
- 2. To provide information about clean power sources
- 3. To promote specific energy companies
- 4. To discuss electricity costs
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To debate environmental policies: The discussion is informative rather than a debate, and focuses on energy rather than broader environmental policies.
✅ 2. To provide information about clean power sources: The entire interview is structured to inform listeners about renewable energy, its types, costs, challenges, and future.
❌ 3. To promote specific energy companies: No specific companies are mentioned or promoted in the content.
❌ 4. To discuss electricity costs: While costs are mentioned, they're just one aspect of the broader informational discussion about renewable energy.

**Q2:** According to the discussion, why are sustainable power sources becoming more popular?
- 1. Government regulations require their use
- 2. They are more reliable than traditional sources
- 3. Technical improvements and lower expenses
- 4. Public demand for cleaner energy
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Government regulations require their use: The content doesn't mention government regulations as a reason for popularity.
❌ 2. They are more reliable than traditional sources: The expert actually mentions intermittency as a concern with renewables.
✅ 3. Technical improvements and lower expenses: The expert specifically states that "Solar and wind have seen significant growth in recent years due to technological advancements and cost reductions."
❌ 4. Public demand for cleaner energy: While this might be true, it's not mentioned in the content as a reason for increased popularity.

**Q3:** What concern is addressed about alternative energy sources?
- 1. Their environmental impact
- 2. Their consistent output capability
- 3. Their installation complexity
- 4. Their maintenance requirements
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Their environmental impact: Environmental concerns about renewables aren't discussed in the content.
✅ 2. Their consistent output capability: The expert directly addresses reliability, stating "While individual sources like solar or wind can be intermittent, a diverse mix of renewables can provide reliable power."
❌ 3. Their installation complexity: Installation complexity isn't mentioned as a concern.
❌ 4. Their maintenance requirements: Maintenance of renewable energy sources isn't discussed in the content.

**Q4:** What is mentioned about the future of sustainable power?
- 1. It will completely replace traditional sources by 2030
- 2. It requires substantial system improvements
- 3. It will mainly focus on solar power
- 4. It depends on international cooperation
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It will completely replace traditional sources by 2030: The content mentions 2050 as a target for 100% renewable electricity, not 2030.
✅ 2. It requires substantial system improvements: The expert states that the transition "will require significant investment in infrastructure and storage technologies."
❌ 3. It will mainly focus on solar power: The content doesn't suggest a focus on any single renewable source.
❌ 4. It depends on international cooperation: While cooperation might be helpful, this isn't specifically mentioned in the content.

**Q5:** What is identified as a major obstacle to implementation?
- 1. Public resistance
- 2. Lack of natural resources
- 3. Workforce transition
- 4. International regulations
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Public resistance: This is not mentioned as a challenge in the content.
❌ 2. Lack of natural resources: The content doesn't mention resource scarcity as an issue.
✅ 3. Workforce transition: The expert specifically mentions "the challenge of transitioning jobs from fossil fuel industries to renewables" as one of the challenges.
❌ 4. International regulations: The content doesn't mention international regulations as a challenge.

**Q6:** What is suggested about the current cost situation?
- 1. All sustainable sources are more expensive
- 2. Initial costs remain a consideration
- 3. Traditional sources are always cheaper
- 4. Costs are the same everywhere
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. All sustainable sources are more expensive: The expert states that "in many areas, wind and solar are now cheaper than coal or gas."
✅ 2. Initial costs remain a consideration: The expert specifically mentions that "the initial setup costs can still be high" even though ongoing costs have decreased.
❌ 3. Traditional sources are always cheaper: The content contradicts this by stating renewables are cheaper in many areas.
❌ 4. Costs are the same everywhere: The expert implies cost variations by saying "in many areas" when discussing where renewables are cheaper.

- 4. Passenger well-being

❌ 3. By canceling space programs: There's no mention of canceling programs due to environmental concerns.
❌ 4. By ignoring environmental concerns: The conversation explicitly states that companies are addressing environmental concerns, not ignoring them.

❌ 4. International cooperation: This is not mentioned as a benefit in the conversation.

## Practice 37: Solar Panel Innovation | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a news item about a significant advancement in renewable energy technology.

### Audio Transcript
Scientists have announced a breakthrough in solar panel technology that could revolutionize renewable energy production. The new panels, developed by a team of international researchers, are 50% more efficient than current models and can generate electricity even on cloudy days. This innovation uses a unique combination of materials that capture a broader spectrum of light. The panels are also more durable and have a longer lifespan, reducing replacement costs. Initial testing shows promising results, with the potential to significantly reduce reliance on fossil fuels. Experts predict that if widely adopted, these panels could meet up to 30% of global energy needs within a decade. The team is now working on scaling up production and expects the panels to be commercially available within two years. This development is seen as a major step towards achieving global climate goals and creating a more sustainable future.

## Practice 38: Aquapolis Ocean Settlement | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a news item about an ambitious underwater city project.

### Audio Transcript
Engineers have unveiled plans for the world's first sustainable underwater city. The project, named Aquapolis, aims to house up to 5,000 residents in a series of interconnected domes on the ocean floor. The city will be powered by a combination of tidal energy and floating solar panels. Oxygen will be produced through seaweed farms surrounding the structure. The project's lead architect emphasized the potential for Aquapolis to alleviate housing shortages in coastal areas and provide a unique living experience. Critics, however, question the feasibility and environmental impact of such a large-scale underwater development. The project is estimated to cost billions and take at least 15 years to complete. If successful, Aquapolis could pave the way for future underwater settlements and revolutionize our approach to urban planning in the face of rising sea levels.

## Practice 39: Classroom AI Adoption | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a news item about the implementation of artificial intelligence in education.

### Audio Transcript
A recent report highlights the growing use of artificial intelligence in education systems worldwide. Schools are increasingly adopting AI-powered tools to personalize learning experiences for students. These systems can adapt to individual learning styles, providing customized content and pacing. Additionally, AI is being used to automate administrative tasks, freeing up teachers to focus more on student interaction. The technology can analyze vast amounts of data to identify learning trends and areas where students may need extra support. However, concerns have been raised about data privacy and the potential for AI to replace human teachers. Proponents argue that AI should be seen as a tool to enhance, not replace, traditional teaching methods. As the technology continues to evolve, education experts stress the importance of balancing innovation with ethical considerations to ensure the best outcomes for students.

### Audio Transcript

These micro-drones, equipped with GPS and artificial intelligence, can navigate fields and pollinate crops just like real bees. Early trials show promising results, with a 60% success rate in pollination. The robots use tiny brushes and adhesive to collect and transfer pollen between plants. Researchers aim to deploy these robotic pollinators alongside conservation efforts for natural bee populations. The technology is particularly valuable for crops in greenhouses and areas with severe pollinator shortages. While not a complete replacement for natural pollinators, these robotic bees offer a supplementary solution to ensure food security. The next phase of development focuses on improving the drones' efficiency and reducing production costs to make them more accessible to farmers worldwide.

## Practice 71: Bicycle Gear Adjustment | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a customer and a bicycle repair shop employee. The customer is seeking advice about a problem with their bicycle.

### Audio Transcript
A: Welcome to Pedal Perfect Bike Shop. How can I assist you today?

B: Hi, I'm having an issue with my bicycle. The gears are not shifting smoothly.

A: I see. Can you describe what happens when you try to change gears?

B: When I shift, there's a delay, and sometimes it makes a grinding noise.

A: That sounds like it could be a problem with the derailleur or cable tension. When was the last time you had a tune-up?

B: It's been about a year, I think.

A: Okay. I'd recommend bringing it in for a full inspection. We can adjust the derailleur and check the cables.

B: How long would that take?

A: Usually about an hour, but we're quite busy today. We could have it ready by tomorrow afternoon.

B: That works for me. What's the cost for this service?

A: A basic tune-up is $60, which includes gear adjustment.

B: Alright, I'll bring it in this afternoon. Thanks for your help.

A: Great, we'll take good care of it. See you later!

**Q1:** What is the customer's main concern?
- 1. The bicycle's brakes are squeaking
- 2. The gears are not working properly
- 3. The tires need replacement
- 4. The chain keeps falling off
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The bicycle's brakes are squeaking: No mention of brake problems in the conversation.
✅ 2. The gears are not working properly: The customer specifically states "The gears are not shifting smoothly" and describes grinding noises.
❌ 3. The tires need replacement: No tire issues are mentioned in the dialogue.
❌ 4. The chain keeps falling off: Chain problems aren't mentioned by the customer.

**Q2:** How long has it been since the customer's last bicycle service?
- 1. Six months
- 2. Two years
- 3. Approximately one year
- 4. The customer doesn't remember
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Six months: This timeframe isn't mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 2. Two years: This timeframe isn't mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 3. Approximately one year: The customer states "It's been about a year, I think" when asked about the last tune-up.
❌ 4. The customer doesn't remember: The customer does provide an approximate timeframe, showing they do remember.

**Q3:** Why can't the shop fix the bicycle immediately?
- 1. They lack the necessary parts
- 2. Their mechanic is away
- 3. They have many customers
- 4. The repair is too complex
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They lack the necessary parts: No mention of missing parts in the conversation.
❌ 2. Their mechanic is away: This reason isn't mentioned in the dialogue.
✅ 3. They have many customers: The employee states "we're quite busy today" as the reason they can't fix it immediately.
❌ 4. The repair is too complex: The complexity of the repair isn't given as a reason for the delay.

**Q4:** What does the shop employee suggest?
- 1. Replacing the entire gear system
- 2. A complete bicycle inspection
- 3. Buying a new bicycle
- 4. Waiting for a few weeks
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Replacing the entire gear system: The employee doesn't suggest a complete replacement.
✅ 2. A complete bicycle inspection: The employee says "I'd recommend bringing it in for a full inspection."
❌ 3. Buying a new bicycle: There's no suggestion to purchase a new bicycle.
❌ 4. Waiting for a few weeks: The employee offers to have it ready by the next afternoon, not weeks later.

**Q5:** How much will the service cost?
- 1. Less than $50
- 2. $$60
- 3. More than $100
- 4. The price wasn't specified
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Less than $50: This price isn't mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. $60: The employee clearly states "A basic tune-up is $60, which includes gear adjustment."
❌ 3. More than $100: This price isn't mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. The price wasn't specified: The price was explicitly mentioned as $60.

**Q6:** What is the customer's response to the service proposal?
- 1. Requests a different service time
- 2. Agrees to bring the bicycle later that day
- 3. Decides to look for another shop
- 4. Asks for a price reduction
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Requests a different service time: The customer doesn't ask for a different time than what was proposed.
✅ 2. Agrees to bring the bicycle later that day: The customer says "Alright, I'll bring it in this afternoon."
❌ 3. Decides to look for another shop: There's no indication the customer wants to go elsewhere.
❌ 4. Asks for a price reduction: The customer accepts the quoted price without negotiating.
