❌ 4. Computer programming methods: Programming methods are not discussed in the conversation.

**Q2:** What distinguishes the discussed technology from traditional systems?
- 1. Its processing speed only
- 2. The ability to handle multiple states at once
- 3. The size of the machinery
- 4. The cost of production
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Its processing speed only: The expert mentions speed but it's not the only distinguishing factor.
✅ 2. The ability to handle multiple states at once: The expert explicitly states that qubits "can exist in multiple states simultaneously," which is the key difference from classical bits.
❌ 3. The size of the machinery: Size is not mentioned as a distinguishing factor in the conversation.
❌ 4. The cost of production: Cost is not discussed as a distinguishing factor between quantum and classical computing.

**Q3:** Which field was NOT mentioned as a potential beneficiary?
- 1. Environmental protection
- 2. Medical research
- 3. Transportation
- 4. Financial services
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Environmental protection: The expert mentions "combating climate change through more efficient resource management."
❌ 2. Medical research: The expert mentions "drug discovery" and "personalized medicine."
✅ 3. Transportation: Transportation was not mentioned anywhere in the conversation as a potential application or beneficiary of quantum computing.
❌ 4. Financial services: The expert mentions "optimize financial models."

**Q4:** What is the current state of development in this field?
- 1. Fully operational systems are widely available
- 2. Development has been abandoned
- 3. Initial systems exist with ongoing improvements needed
- 4. Only theoretical models have been created
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Fully operational systems are widely available: The expert indicates they're still working on improvements, not that systems are fully operational or widely available.
❌ 2. Development has been abandoned: The expert describes ongoing progress, not abandonment.
✅ 3. Initial systems exist with ongoing improvements needed: The expert states "Several companies and research institutions have already built small-scale quantum computers. However, we're still working on increasing the number of qubits and reducing error rates."
❌ 4. Only theoretical models have been created: The expert mentions actual small-scale quantum computers have been built, not just theoretical models.

**Q5:** What is described as the primary obstacle?
- 1. Finding qualified researchers
- 2. Securing funding
- 3. Environmental interference
- 4. Public acceptance
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Finding qualified researchers: This was not mentioned as a challenge in the conversation.
❌ 2. Securing funding: Funding was not discussed as a challenge in developing quantum computers.
✅ 3. Environmental interference: The expert states "The biggest challenge is maintaining quantum coherence. Qubits are extremely sensitive to their environment, and any disturbance can cause them to lose their quantum properties."
❌ 4. Public acceptance: This was not mentioned as a challenge in the conversation.

**Q6:** According to the discussion, what future impact is suggested?
- 1. Limited effect on everyday life
- 2. Widespread changes across multiple sectors
- 3. Improvements in gaming only
- 4. Reduced need for technology
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Limited effect on everyday life: The expert suggests significant impacts across multiple areas, not limited effects.
✅ 2. Widespread changes across multiple sectors: The expert mentions impacts in medicine, weather forecasting, AI, internet security, and materials science, indicating widespread changes.
❌ 3. Improvements in gaming only: Gaming was not mentioned as a potential application.
❌ 4. Reduced need for technology: The conversation suggests expanded uses of technology, not reduced need.

- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Headaches: Headaches are not specifically mentioned as a health issue in the conversation.

❌ 3. Back pain: Back pain is not mentioned as a potential health issue in the conversation.
❌ 4. Hand strain: Hand strain is not mentioned as a potential health issue in the conversation.

**Q6:** What is implied about traditional teaching?
- 1. It will still have value
- 2. It is becoming obsolete
- 3. It needs complete revision
- 4. It is too expensive

- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. It will still have value: The expert explicitly states that "VR should complement, not replace, traditional teaching methods," indicating traditional teaching will continue to have value.

## Practice 132: Router Connection Troubleshooting | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman regarding an internet connectivity issue.

### Audio Transcript
WOMAN: Hello, technical support. How can I help you today?
MAN: Hi, I'm having trouble with my internet connection. It keeps dropping every few minutes.
WOMAN: I understand how frustrating that can be. Let me help you troubleshoot. First, could you tell me if all devices are experiencing this issue?
MAN: Yes, both my laptop and phone keep losing connection.
WOMAN: Okay, and have you tried restarting your router?
MAN: Actually, no. Should I do that now?
WOMAN: Yes, please. Unplug your router from the power source, wait for 30 seconds, then plug it back in. It will take about two minutes to fully restart.
MAN: Alright, I'll do that now... Okay, it's back on.
WOMAN: Perfect. Let's wait a few minutes to see if the connection stabilizes. While we wait, can you confirm your account details with me?
MAN: Sure, my account number is 45892.

**Q1:** Why is the man calling technical support?
- 1. His router is not working at all
- 2. He wants to upgrade his internet speed
- 3. He is experiencing intermittent connection issues
- 4. He forgot his Wi-Fi password
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. His router is not working at all: The man states his internet "keeps dropping every few minutes," indicating intermittent issues, not complete failure.
❌ 2. He wants to upgrade his internet speed: The man calls about connection problems, not to request an upgrade.
✅ 3. He is experiencing intermittent connection issues: The man explicitly states "I'm having trouble with my internet connection. It keeps dropping every few minutes."
❌ 4. He forgot his Wi-Fi password: No mention of password issues in the conversation.

**Q2:** What is the first solution the woman suggests?
- 1. Checking account details
- 2. Upgrading the router
- 3. Restarting the router
- 4. Calling a technician
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Checking account details: This happens later in the conversation while waiting for the router to restart.
❌ 2. Upgrading the router: This is suggested after the initial troubleshooting.
✅ 3. Restarting the router: The woman's first troubleshooting step is "have you tried restarting your router?" and then instructs him to do so.
❌ 4. Calling a technician: The technician visit is arranged after the router restart, as a solution for the ongoing issues.

### Audio Transcript
WOMAN: How's the connection now?
MAN: It seems better, but I'm still concerned. This has been happening a lot lately.
WOMAN: I can see from your account that you're using our older model router. We've been upgrading customers to our new Wi-Fi 6 router for free. Would you be interested?
MAN: That sounds good, but what's the catch?
WOMAN: No catch! It's a complimentary upgrade for loyal customers. The new router has better range and stability. We can have a technician bring it tomorrow.
MAN: That would be great. What time can they come?
WOMAN: Let me check our schedule... We have slots at 9 AM or 2 PM.
MAN: I'll take the 9 AM slot.
WOMAN: Perfect. I've scheduled that for you. The technician will call before arriving.

**Q3:** What does the company offer the man?
- 1. A discounted router upgrade
- 2. A complimentary router replacement
- 3. A faster internet package
- 4. A free technical consultation
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A discounted router upgrade: The upgrade is offered for free, not at a discount.
✅ 2. A complimentary router replacement: The woman states "We've been upgrading customers to our new Wi-Fi 6 router for free" and confirms it's a "complimentary upgrade for loyal customers."
❌ 3. A faster internet package: The conversation only mentions a router upgrade, not a change in internet speed package.
❌ 4. A free technical consultation: While technical support is provided, the specific offer is for a new router.

**Q4:** What time will the technician visit?
- 1. Early afternoon
- 2. Morning
- 3. Evening
- 4. Whenever the customer prefers
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Early afternoon: The woman offers a 2 PM slot, which would be early afternoon, but the man specifically chooses the morning option.
✅ 2. Morning: The woman offers a 9 AM slot which the man accepts when he says "I'll take the 9 AM slot."
❌ 3. Evening: No evening appointment options were mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. Whenever the customer prefers: The woman offers specific time slots (9 AM or 2 PM), not an open choice.

**Q5:** What is implied about the company's service?
- 1. They prioritize new customers
- 2. They charge for all upgrades
- 3. They value customer retention
- 4. They have limited technician availability
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They prioritize new customers: The conversation suggests they value existing customers by offering free upgrades to "loyal customers."
❌ 2. They charge for all upgrades: The woman explicitly states the router upgrade is "complimentary" and "free."
✅ 3. They value customer retention: The woman refers to the free upgrade being for "loyal customers," implying they value keeping their existing customers.
❌ 4. They have limited technician availability: The woman offers multiple appointment options, suggesting reasonable availability.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: One more thing - will I need to back up any settings before the switch?
WOMAN: No need to worry about that. The technician will transfer all your current settings to the new router. However, you might want to write down your current Wi-Fi password just in case.
MAN: Got it. And how long will the installation take?
WOMAN: The whole process usually takes about 30 minutes. The technician will also make sure everything is working properly before leaving.
MAN: That's good to know. Will they show me how to use the new router?
WOMAN: Absolutely! They'll demonstrate all the new features and leave you with a user guide. Is there anything else you'd like to know?
MAN: No, that covers everything. Thanks for your help!
WOMAN: You're welcome! Have a great day!

**Q6:** What does the woman say about the installation process?
- 1. The customer needs to back up all settings
- 2. It requires customer preparation
- 3. It will take several hours
- 4. The technician handles everything
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The customer needs to back up all settings: The woman specifically says "No need to worry about that. The technician will transfer all your current settings."
❌ 2. It requires customer preparation: The woman indicates no preparation is needed except possibly writing down the Wi-Fi password as a precaution.
❌ 3. It will take several hours: The woman states "The whole process usually takes about 30 minutes."
✅ 4. The technician handles everything: The woman confirms the technician will transfer all settings, ensure everything works properly, and demonstrate the features.

**Q7:** What will the technician do after installation?
- 1. Schedule a follow-up visit
- 2. Provide training and documentation
- 3. Change the Wi-Fi password
- 4. Test only the basic features
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Schedule a follow-up visit: No follow-up visit is mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. Provide training and documentation: The woman states "They'll demonstrate all the new features and leave you with a user guide."
❌ 3. Change the Wi-Fi password: The woman suggests writing down the current password as a precaution, but doesn't say it will be changed.
❌ 4. Test only the basic features: The woman says the technician "will also make sure everything is working properly," not just basic features.

**Q8:** What is suggested about the new router?
- 1. It requires complex setup
- 2. It has additional features
- 3. It costs more to maintain
- 4. It needs frequent updates
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It requires complex setup: The conversation indicates the technician will handle the setup completely.
✅ 2. It has additional features: The woman mentions the technician will "demonstrate all the new features" and that the router has "better range and stability."
❌ 3. It costs more to maintain: No information about maintenance costs is mentioned.
❌ 4. It needs frequent updates: No information about updates is mentioned in the conversation.

## Practice 133: Dental Checkup Rescheduling | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a dental clinic. The woman is a receptionist, and the man is a patient trying to reschedule his appointment.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, I'm calling about my dental appointment scheduled for tomorrow morning at 10 AM. I'm afraid I need to reschedule.
WOMAN: Of course, I can help you with that. Could you please provide your name?
MAN: Yes, it's Michael Thompson.
WOMAN: Thank you, Mr. Thompson. I see your appointment here with Dr. Roberts for a routine cleaning and check-up. May I ask why you need to reschedule?
MAN: I just found out I have an important work meeting that I can't miss. I was wondering if there's any availability later this week?
WOMAN: Let me check our schedule. We have an opening on Thursday at 2 PM, or Friday morning at 11:30 AM.
MAN: Thursday afternoon would work better for me.
WOMAN: Perfect, I'll put you down for Thursday at 2 PM with Dr. Roberts. Please remember to arrive 15 minutes early to complete any necessary paperwork.

**Q1:** Why does the man initially call?
- 1. To cancel his appointment completely
- 2. To confirm his appointment time
- 3. To change his appointment time
- 4. To make a new appointment
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To cancel his appointment completely: He wants to reschedule, not cancel.
❌ 2. To confirm his appointment time: He already knows his appointment time.
✅ 3. To change his appointment time: He says 'I'm calling about my dental appointment scheduled for tomorrow morning at 10 AM. I'm afraid I need to reschedule.'
❌ 4. To make a new appointment: He already has an appointment scheduled.

**Q2:** What type of appointment does the man have scheduled?
- 1. A root canal procedure
- 2. A routine cleaning and check-up
- 3. An emergency dental procedure
- 4. A dental consultation
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A root canal procedure: This type of appointment is not mentioned.
✅ 2. A routine cleaning and check-up: The receptionist specifically states 'I see your appointment here with Dr. Roberts for a routine cleaning and check-up.'
❌ 3. An emergency dental procedure: This type of appointment is not mentioned.
❌ 4. A dental consultation: This type of appointment is not mentioned.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Sorry, I need to call back about that Thursday appointment we just scheduled.
WOMAN: Yes, Mr. Thompson. Is there a problem?
MAN: I just checked my calendar again and realized I have a dentist phobia seminar that afternoon. I completely forgot about it.
WOMAN: I understand. These things happen. Would you prefer the Friday morning slot I mentioned earlier?
MAN: Actually, I was wondering if you have anything available next week? I'd prefer a morning appointment if possible.
WOMAN: Let me look at next week's schedule. We have Tuesday at 9 AM or Wednesday at 8:30 AM.
MAN: The Wednesday appointment would be perfect.
WOMAN: Great, I'll schedule you for Wednesday at 8:30 AM. Just to remind you, we require 24-hour notice for any future cancellations.

**Q3:** What is the reason for the man's second call?
- 1. He has another dental appointment
- 2. He has a scheduling conflict with a seminar
- 3. He wants an earlier appointment
- 4. He needs to cancel completely
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He has another dental appointment: The man doesn't mention having another dental appointment.
✅ 2. He has a scheduling conflict with a seminar: The man says 'I just checked my calendar again and realized I have a dentist phobia seminar that afternoon.'
❌ 3. He wants an earlier appointment: He doesn't specifically request an earlier time, just a different day.
❌ 4. He needs to cancel completely: He wants to reschedule, not cancel completely.

**Q4:** What new requirement does the receptionist mention?
- 1. Payment must be made in advance
- 2. Insurance information is needed
- 3. A 24-hour cancellation notice
- 4. A medical history update
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Payment must be made in advance: This requirement is not mentioned.
❌ 2. Insurance information is needed: This requirement is not mentioned.
✅ 3. A 24-hour cancellation notice: The receptionist says 'Just to remind you, we require 24-hour notice for any future cancellations.'
❌ 4. A medical history update: This requirement is not mentioned.

**Q5:** Which time slot does the man choose in the second conversation?
- 1. Tuesday at 9 AM
- 2. Thursday at 2 PM
- 3. Friday at 11:30 AM
- 4. Wednesday at 8:30 AM
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Tuesday at 9 AM: This was offered but not chosen.
❌ 2. Thursday at 2 PM: This was chosen in the first conversation, not the second.
❌ 3. Friday at 11:30 AM: This was offered but not chosen.
✅ 4. Wednesday at 8:30 AM: The man says 'The Wednesday appointment would be perfect' when offered Wednesday at 8:30 AM.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, it's Michael Thompson again. I'm really sorry about this, but I need to make one final change.
WOMAN: Hello, Mr. Thompson. I see you're currently scheduled for Wednesday at 8:30 AM.
MAN: Yes, but I just found out my car needs to go to the mechanic that morning. Would it be possible to get an afternoon appointment instead?
WOMAN: Let me see what we have available. We do have a cancellation for Wednesday at 3:15 PM.
MAN: That would work perfectly! I promise this will be the last change.
WOMAN: No problem, Mr. Thompson. I've moved you to Wednesday at 3:15 PM. Just to confirm, you'll need to be here by 3 PM, and the appointment will take about an hour.
MAN: Thank you so much for your patience. I really appreciate it.
WOMAN: You're welcome. We'll see you on Wednesday afternoon.

**Q6:** What is the reason for the man's final schedule change?
- 1. A work commitment
- 2. Car maintenance
- 3. Another dental appointment
- 4. Personal preference
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A work commitment: This was the reason for his first change, not the final one.
✅ 2. Car maintenance: The man states 'I just found out my car needs to go to the mechanic that morning.'
❌ 3. Another dental appointment: He doesn't mention having another dental appointment.
❌ 4. Personal preference: He gives a specific reason (car maintenance), not just a preference.

**Q7:** What is the final appointment time agreed upon?
- 1. Wednesday at 3:00 PM
- 2. Wednesday at 3:15 PM
- 3. Wednesday at 4:15 PM
- 4. Wednesday at 2:15 PM
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Wednesday at 3:00 PM: This is when he needs to arrive, not the appointment time.
✅ 2. Wednesday at 3:15 PM: The receptionist says 'I've moved you to Wednesday at 3:15 PM.'
❌ 3. Wednesday at 4:15 PM: This time is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. Wednesday at 2:15 PM: This time is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q8:** What can be inferred about the receptionist's attitude?
- 1. She is frustrated with the changes
- 2. She is professional and accommodating
- 3. She is rushing to end the conversation
- 4. She is new to her job
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She is frustrated with the changes: She shows no signs of frustration throughout the conversation.
✅ 2. She is professional and accommodating: She helps the man reschedule multiple times without complaint and responds with 'No problem' and 'You're welcome.'
❌ 3. She is rushing to end the conversation: She takes time to provide details and doesn't try to end the call quickly.
❌ 4. She is new to her job: There's no indication she's new; she handles the scheduling efficiently.

## Practice 134: Study Abroad Advice | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a university's international office. The woman is a study abroad advisor, and the man is a student seeking information.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, I'm interested in applying for a study abroad program next semester. Could you help me understand the process?
WOMAN: Of course! Are you looking at any specific countries or programs?
MAN: I'm thinking about studying in Japan. I'm majoring in Business, and I've heard they have some great programs there.
WOMAN: Yes, we have partnerships with several universities in Japan. Have you checked our website? There's detailed information about each program.
MAN: Actually, I did, but I'm a bit confused about the requirements. It says I need a minimum GPA of 3.0 and language proficiency.
WOMAN: That's right. For Japan, you'll need either two years of Japanese language courses or a JLPT N2 certification. What's your current situation with the language?
MAN: I've completed one year of Japanese, but I'm not sure if that's enough.
WOMAN: In that case, you might want to consider our English-taught programs in Japan. Several partner universities offer business courses in English.

**Q1:** What is the main purpose of the man's visit?
- 1. To learn Japanese language
- 2. To inquire about study abroad programs
- 3. To submit program application
- 4. To get a scholarship
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To learn Japanese language: While language is discussed, it's not the purpose of his visit.
✅ 2. To inquire about study abroad programs: The man begins by saying "I'm interested in applying for a study abroad program next semester. Could you help me understand the process?"
❌ 3. To submit program application: He's gathering information, not submitting an application yet.
❌ 4. To get a scholarship: While scholarships are discussed, they're not the primary purpose of his visit.

**Q2:** What is the man's current situation regarding Japanese language?
- 1. He has JLPT N2 certification
- 2. He has completed two years of Japanese
- 3. He has completed one year of Japanese
- 4. He has no Japanese language background
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He has JLPT N2 certification: He never mentions having this certification.
❌ 2. He has completed two years of Japanese: The requirement is two years, but he hasn't completed this.
✅ 3. He has completed one year of Japanese: The man specifically states "I've completed one year of Japanese, but I'm not sure if that's enough."
❌ 4. He has no Japanese language background: This contradicts his statement about completing one year of Japanese.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: That sounds perfect! What about the application timeline? When should I start preparing?
WOMAN: For spring semester programs, the deadline is October 1st, which is about two months away. You'll need to submit several documents.
MAN: What kind of documents do I need?
WOMAN: You'll need your transcript, two letters of recommendation from professors, a statement of purpose, and a study plan. Also, don't forget your passport copy.
MAN: Oh, I actually need to renew my passport. How long does the whole application process usually take?
WOMAN: From submission to acceptance, it typically takes about six weeks. But you should start gathering documents now, especially the recommendations.
MAN: That makes sense. Do you have any tips for writing the statement of purpose?
WOMAN: Yes, focus on how studying in Japan aligns with your academic and career goals. I can review your draft if you'd like.

**Q3:** When is the application deadline for spring semester?
- 1. September 10th
- 2. October 1st
- 3. In six weeks
- 4. In two months
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. September 10th: This is the scholarship deadline, not the program application deadline.
✅ 2. October 1st: The woman specifically states "For spring semester programs, the deadline is October 1st."
❌ 3. In six weeks: This is how long the application process typically takes, not the deadline.
❌ 4. In two months: The woman mentions the deadline is "about two months away," but this isn't the actual deadline date.

**Q4:** Which document is NOT mentioned as required for the application?
- 1. Transcript
- 2. Letters of recommendation
- 3. Financial statement
- 4. Study plan
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Transcript: The woman specifically lists transcript as a required document.
❌ 2. Letters of recommendation: The woman mentions needing "two letters of recommendation from professors."
✅ 3. Financial statement: This document is never mentioned in the conversation as being required.
❌ 4. Study plan: The woman specifically lists study plan as a required document.

**Q5:** What does the woman offer to do for the man?
- 1. Help write his statement of purpose
- 2. Review his statement draft
- 3. Submit his application
- 4. Arrange professor recommendations
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Help write his statement of purpose: She offers to review it, not write it.
✅ 2. Review his statement draft: The woman specifically says "I can review your draft if you'd like" when discussing the statement of purpose.
❌ 3. Submit his application: She never offers to submit his application for him.
❌ 4. Arrange professor recommendations: She mentions recommendations are needed but doesn't offer to arrange them.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Thanks, that would be really helpful. What about the financial aspects? Are there scholarships available?
WOMAN: Yes, we have several scholarship options. The Global Education Fund offers up to $5,000, and there are Japan-specific scholarships too.
MAN: When are the scholarship deadlines?
WOMAN: Most scholarship applications are due three weeks before the program deadline. So for spring semester, that would be September 10th.
MAN: That's coming up pretty soon. Could you help me understand the living costs in Japan?
WOMAN: Of course. Housing in university dorms runs about $500-700 per month, and you should budget another $600 for food and transportation.
MAN: That's actually less than I expected. Do you have any information packets I can take home?
WOMAN: Yes, here's our Japan program guide. It has everything we discussed, plus contact information for former participants you can talk to.

**Q6:** How much scholarship money is available through the Global Education Fund?
- 1. Up to $5,000
- 2. Up to $500
- 3. Up to $700
- 4. Up to $600
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. Up to $5,000: The woman explicitly states "The Global Education Fund offers up to $5,000."
❌ 2. Up to $500: This amount is mentioned as the lower end of housing costs, not scholarship money.
❌ 3. Up to $700: This amount is mentioned as the higher end of housing costs, not scholarship money.
❌ 4. Up to $600: This amount is mentioned for food and transportation budget, not scholarship money.

**Q7:** What is the estimated monthly cost for housing in Japan?
- 1. $$500 only
- 2. $$600 only
- 3. $$700 only
- 4. $$500-700
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. $500 only: This is incomplete as the woman mentions a range, not just $500.
❌ 2. $600 only: This amount is mentioned for food and transportation, not housing.
❌ 3. $700 only: This is incomplete as the woman mentions a range, not just $700.
✅ 4. $500-700: The woman specifically states "Housing in university dorms runs about $500-700 per month."

**Q8:** What additional resource does the woman provide at the end?
- 1. Scholarship application forms
- 2. Language proficiency guidelines
- 3. Japan program guide with alumni contacts
- 4. Housing application forms
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Scholarship application forms: These aren't mentioned as being provided at the end.
❌ 2. Language proficiency guidelines: These aren't mentioned as being provided at the end.
✅ 3. Japan program guide with alumni contacts: The woman says "here's our Japan program guide. It has everything we discussed, plus contact information for former participants you can talk to."
❌ 4. Housing application forms: These aren't mentioned as being provided at the end.

## Practice 135: Family Health Plan | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a health insurance office. The woman is an insurance representative, and the man is inquiring about health coverage.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Good morning. I'm here to inquire about health insurance coverage. I recently started my own business and need coverage for myself and my family.
WOMAN: Welcome! I'd be happy to help you explore our insurance plans. How many family members need coverage?
MAN: It would be for myself, my wife, and our two children, ages 8 and 12.
WOMAN: Perfect. We have several family plans available. Are you looking for comprehensive coverage or a basic plan?
MAN: Well, my daughter has asthma, so we definitely need something that covers prescription medications and specialist visits.
WOMAN: I understand. Our Gold Family Plan might be the best fit. It includes prescription coverage, specialist visits with a small copay, and preventive care at no additional cost.
MAN: That sounds promising. What would the monthly premium be for that plan?
WOMAN: For a family of four, the Gold Plan premium would be $850 per month. However, depending on your income, you might qualify for subsidies that could reduce that amount.

**Q1:** What does the man need?
- 1. Coverage for his employees
- 2. A basic insurance plan
- 3. Family health insurance
- 4. Individual health coverage
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Coverage for his employees: The man doesn't mention needing coverage for employees.
❌ 2. A basic insurance plan: The man specifically needs coverage for his daughter's asthma, requiring more than a basic plan.
✅ 3. Family health insurance: The man explicitly states he needs coverage for himself, his wife, and their two children.
❌ 4. Individual health coverage: The man needs coverage for his entire family, not just himself.

**Q2:** Why does the woman suggest the Gold Family Plan?
- 1. It's the cheapest option available
- 2. It covers pre-existing conditions
- 3. It includes preventive care and prescriptions
- 4. The man specifically requested it
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It's the cheapest option available: The conversation doesn't indicate this is the cheapest plan.
❌ 2. It covers pre-existing conditions: While the plan does cover pre-existing conditions, the woman states that all their plans cover pre-existing conditions.
✅ 3. It includes preventive care and prescriptions: The woman specifically recommends this plan because it includes prescription coverage and specialist visits, which addresses the man's concern about his daughter's asthma.
❌ 4. The man specifically requested it: The man didn't request this plan; the woman suggested it based on his needs.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: I see. Could you tell me more about these subsidies? How do I know if I qualify?
WOMAN: Of course. The subsidies are based on your household income and size. Do you have an estimate of your annual household income?
MAN: Well, since I just started my business, it's a bit hard to predict, but I'm estimating around $75,000 this year.
WOMAN: Let me check our subsidy calculator... Based on that income and your family size, you could qualify for about $200 in monthly subsidies.
MAN: That would definitely help. What about deductibles and out-of-pocket maximums?
WOMAN: The Gold Plan has a $2,000 family deductible and a $6,000 out-of-pocket maximum per year. Once you reach that maximum, all covered services are paid at 100%.
MAN: And what about pre-existing conditions like my daughter's asthma?
WOMAN: Don't worry, all our plans cover pre-existing conditions from day one of coverage.

**Q3:** What determines the subsidy amount?
- 1. The type of insurance plan chosen
- 2. The family's medical conditions
- 3. The household size and income
- 4. The business type
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The type of insurance plan chosen: The plan type doesn't determine subsidies according to the conversation.
❌ 2. The family's medical conditions: Medical conditions aren't mentioned as factors for subsidies.
✅ 3. The household size and income: The woman explicitly states "The subsidies are based on your household income and size."
❌ 4. The business type: The type of business isn't mentioned as a factor for determining subsidies.

**Q4:** What is the annual family deductible for the Gold Plan?
- 1. $$850
- 2. $$2,000
- 3. $$6,000
- 4. $$75,000
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. $850: This is the monthly premium, not the deductible.
✅ 2. $2,000: The woman specifically states "The Gold Plan has a $2,000 family deductible."
❌ 3. $6,000: This is the out-of-pocket maximum, not the deductible.
❌ 4. $75,000: This is the man's estimated annual income, not related to the deductible.

**Q5:** Which statement is true about pre-existing conditions?
- 1. They require additional paperwork
- 2. They are covered immediately
- 3. They have higher copayments
- 4. They need special approval
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They require additional paperwork: This isn't mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. They are covered immediately: The woman states "all our plans cover pre-existing conditions from day one of coverage."
❌ 3. They have higher copayments: Higher copayments for pre-existing conditions aren't mentioned.
❌ 4. They need special approval: Special approval for pre-existing conditions isn't mentioned.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: That's good to know. One more thing - what's the enrollment process like?
WOMAN: It's quite straightforward. We'll need to complete an application form with your family's information, and you'll need to provide proof of income and residency.
MAN: How long does it typically take to get approved?
WOMAN: Once we have all the required documents, approval usually takes 3-5 business days. Coverage can start on the first of the next month.
MAN: Perfect. And what documents do I need for proof of income since I'm self-employed?
WOMAN: You can provide your most recent tax return, bank statements, or profit and loss statements from your business. We can be flexible since your business is new.
MAN: Alright, I think I'd like to proceed with applying for the Gold Plan. Should we start the paperwork now?
WOMAN: Absolutely! Let me grab the application forms, and we can get started right away.

**Q6:** How long will it take to process the application?
- 1. 1-2 business days
- 2. 3-5 business days
- 3. One month
- 4. Immediately
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 1-2 business days: This timeframe isn't mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. 3-5 business days: The woman specifically states "approval usually takes 3-5 business days."
❌ 3. One month: This timeframe isn't mentioned; the woman says coverage can start on the first of the next month, but approval is faster.
❌ 4. Immediately: The woman indicates there is a processing time, not immediate approval.

**Q7:** What documentation is NOT mentioned as acceptable proof of income?
- 1. Tax returns
- 2. Bank statements
- 3. Pay stubs
- 4. Profit and loss statements
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Tax returns: The woman specifically mentions tax returns as acceptable proof of income.
❌ 2. Bank statements: Bank statements are mentioned as acceptable documentation.
✅ 3. Pay stubs: Pay stubs are never mentioned in the conversation as acceptable proof of income.
❌ 4. Profit and loss statements: The woman explicitly mentions profit and loss statements as acceptable documentation.

**Q8:** What will most likely happen next?
- 1. The man will think about it more
- 2. They will complete the application
- 3. The man will shop around more
- 4. They will discuss other plans
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The man will think about it more: The man has already decided to proceed with the Gold Plan.
✅ 2. They will complete the application: The man says "I'd like to proceed with applying for the Gold Plan" and the woman responds "Let me grab the application forms, and we can get started right away."
❌ 3. The man will shop around more: The man has already decided to proceed with this plan.
❌ 4. They will discuss other plans: The man has already decided on the Gold Plan and wants to start the paperwork.

## Practice 136: Moving Service Quote | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a moving company office. The woman is a moving service representative, and the man is a customer requesting a quote.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi there, I'm looking to get a quote for moving services next month.
WOMAN: Welcome! I'd be happy to help you with that. Do you know your moving date yet?
MAN: Yes, I'm planning to move on the 15th of next month. I'm relocating from my apartment to a house about 20 miles away.
WOMAN: Perfect, let me check our availability for that date. Could you tell me the size of your current place?
MAN: It's a two-bedroom apartment on the third floor, about 1,000 square feet.
WOMAN: I see. And does your building have an elevator?
MAN: Unfortunately not, that's one of the reasons I'm hiring professionals.
WOMAN: That's definitely something we'll factor into the quote. We'll need to assign extra crew members for stairs. Would you like packing services as well?
MAN: No, I think I can handle the packing myself. I just need help with the heavy lifting and transportation.

**Q1:** What is the main reason the man is seeking professional movers?
- 1. He has too many items to move
- 2. He needs help with packing
- 3. The building lacks an elevator
- 4. He is moving a long distance
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He has too many items to move: The number of items isn't mentioned as a concern.
❌ 2. He needs help with packing: He explicitly declines packing services, saying 'I think I can handle the packing myself.'
✅ 3. The building lacks an elevator: When asked about an elevator, the man states 'Unfortunately not, that's one of the reasons I'm hiring professionals.'
❌ 4. He is moving a long distance: The man mentions he's moving 'about 20 miles away,' which isn't considered a long-distance move.

**Q2:** What service did the man decline?
- 1. Insurance coverage
- 2. Packing assistance
- 3. Transportation
- 4. Heavy lifting
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Insurance coverage: The man actually agrees to insurance, including additional coverage for the piano.
✅ 2. Packing assistance: The man explicitly states 'No, I think I can handle the packing myself. I just need help with the heavy lifting and transportation.'
❌ 3. Transportation: The man specifically says he needs help with transportation.
❌ 4. Heavy lifting: The man specifically says he needs help with heavy lifting.

### Audio Transcript
WOMAN: Alright, I'll need some more details about your furniture. Do you have any particularly large or valuable items?
MAN: Yes, I have a grand piano that needs special handling. I'm quite concerned about moving it safely.
WOMAN: Don't worry, we have extensive experience with piano moving. We'll need to send our specialty team for that. They use specific equipment and techniques.
MAN: That's reassuring. I also have a large sectional sofa that might be tricky to move down the stairs.
WOMAN: We can handle that too. Will you need storage services at all?
MAN: No, I'm moving directly to the new house. But I was wondering about insurance coverage.
WOMAN: We provide basic insurance coverage included in the quote, but I recommend additional coverage for the piano.
MAN: Yes, that makes sense. Could you include that in the quote?

**Q3:** What requires special handling during the move?
- 1. A sectional sofa
- 2. A grand piano
- 3. Valuable artwork
- 4. Heavy furniture
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A sectional sofa: While the sofa is mentioned as potentially tricky to move, it doesn't require special handling beyond normal moving procedures.
✅ 2. A grand piano: The woman specifically mentions they'll 'need to send our specialty team for that. They use specific equipment and techniques' for the piano.
❌ 3. Valuable artwork: No artwork is mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. Heavy furniture: While heavy furniture is mentioned, no special handling beyond normal moving procedures is specified.

**Q4:** What additional service did the woman recommend?
- 1. Storage service
- 2. Extra insurance for the piano
- 3. Additional moving crew
- 4. Packing service
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Storage service: Storage was mentioned but not recommended; the man said he didn't need it.
✅ 2. Extra insurance for the piano: The woman specifically states 'I recommend additional coverage for the piano.'
❌ 3. Additional moving crew: While extra crew for stairs is mentioned, this is presented as a necessity, not a recommendation.
❌ 4. Packing service: Packing was offered but not recommended; the man declined this service.

**Q5:** What is included in the basic moving service?
- 1. Storage
- 2. Packing
- 3. Insurance coverage
- 4. Parking permits
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Storage: The man says he doesn't need storage services.
❌ 2. Packing: The man declines packing services, indicating it's not part of the basic service.
✅ 3. Insurance coverage: The woman states 'We provide basic insurance coverage included in the quote' before recommending additional coverage for the piano.
❌ 4. Parking permits: Parking permits are mentioned as something that needs to be arranged separately, not as part of the basic service.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: I just remembered something else - there's limited parking at both locations.
WOMAN: Thanks for mentioning that. We'll need to arrange for parking permits. Do you know if there are any time restrictions?
MAN: The current apartment has a loading zone we can use, but only between 9 AM and 4 PM.
WOMAN: That's helpful information. I'll make sure to schedule your move within those hours. We typically start early morning for moves of this size.
MAN: Perfect. Oh, and what about payment? Do you require a deposit?
WOMAN: Yes, we require a 25% deposit to secure the date, with the balance due after the move is completed.
MAN: That works for me. How long will it take to get the quote?
WOMAN: I can have it ready for you within the hour. Would you like me to email it to you?
MAN: Yes, please. My email is john.smith@email.com.

**Q6:** What constraint affects the moving schedule?
- 1. The movers' availability
- 2. Loading zone time restrictions
- 3. The size of the move
- 4. Parking permit regulations
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The movers' availability: While the woman checks availability, this isn't mentioned as a constraint.
✅ 2. Loading zone time restrictions: The man specifically mentions 'The current apartment has a loading zone we can use, but only between 9 AM and 4 PM' and the woman responds that she'll schedule within those hours.
❌ 3. The size of the move: While the size affects the duration, it doesn't constrain the schedule.
❌ 4. Parking permit regulations: While parking permits are mentioned, specific regulations that would constrain the schedule aren't discussed.

**Q7:** How much deposit is required?
- 1. 50% of total cost
- 2. Full payment upfront
- 3. 25% of total cost
- 4. No deposit required
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 50% of total cost: This percentage is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 2. Full payment upfront: The woman states that the balance is due after the move is completed.
✅ 3. 25% of total cost: The woman clearly states 'we require a 25% deposit to secure the date, with the balance due after the move is completed.'
❌ 4. No deposit required: The conversation explicitly mentions a deposit requirement.

**Q8:** What will happen next in this conversation?
- 1. The man will pay the deposit
- 2. The woman will send a quote
- 3. They will schedule the move
- 4. They will discuss insurance options
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The man will pay the deposit: The deposit is only required after receiving and approving the quote, which hasn't happened yet.
❌ 2. The woman will send a quote: The woman explicitly states 'I can have it ready for you within the hour. Would you like me to email it to you?' and the man agrees.
✅ 3. They will schedule the move: The date is already set for the 15th of next month, but they need the quote first.
❌ 4. They will discuss insurance options: They already discussed insurance, with the woman recommending additional coverage for the piano.

## Practice 137: Wildlife Tour Inquiry | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a wildlife photography tour company. The woman is a tour guide, and the man is a potential customer.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi there, I saw your ad about wildlife photography tours. Could you tell me more about them?
WOMAN: Of course! We offer guided tours in various national parks where you can photograph wildlife in their natural habitat. Are you an experienced photographer?
MAN: Well, I just got my first DSLR camera last month. I've been practicing, but I'm still learning.
WOMAN: That's perfectly fine! Our tours cater to all skill levels. We actually have a beginner-friendly tour coming up next weekend at Cedar Valley Park. It includes basic photography instruction.
MAN: That sounds perfect! What kind of wildlife might we see there?
WOMAN: Cedar Valley is known for its diverse bird species, deer, and if we're lucky, some foxes. Spring is actually the best time to visit because many animals are active with their young.
MAN: That's exactly what I'm looking for. What's the duration of the tour?
WOMAN: It's a full-day tour, from sunrise to sunset, with breaks in between. We provide transportation from the city center.

**Q1:** What attracted the man to contact the woman?
- 1. A photography exhibition
- 2. A wildlife magazine article
- 3. An advertisement for tours
- 4. A friend's recommendation
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A photography exhibition: No exhibition is mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 2. A wildlife magazine article: No magazine article is mentioned.
✅ 3. An advertisement for tours: The man begins the conversation with 'I saw your ad about wildlife photography tours.'
❌ 4. A friend's recommendation: No friend's recommendation is mentioned in the conversation.

**Q2:** Why is the upcoming tour suitable for the man?
- 1. It's the cheapest option available
- 2. It includes photography instruction
- 3. It focuses only on bird photography
- 4. It's shorter than other tours
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It's the cheapest option available: No comparison of prices between different tours is mentioned.
✅ 2. It includes photography instruction: The woman says the tour 'includes basic photography instruction,' which is suitable for the man who mentions he's 'still learning.'
❌ 3. It focuses only on bird photography: The tour includes various wildlife, not just birds. The woman mentions 'diverse bird species, deer, and if we're lucky, some foxes.'
❌ 4. It's shorter than other tours: No comparison of tour durations is mentioned in the conversation.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: I'd like to book the Cedar Valley tour. What equipment should I bring?
WOMAN: Great choice! Besides your camera, I'd recommend bringing a tripod and a zoom lens if you have one. We can rent you equipment if needed.
MAN: I have a basic tripod, but my lens only goes up to 200mm. Will that be enough?
WOMAN: That should work for most situations, but we do have some 400mm lenses available for rent at $50 per day.
MAN: I might want to rent one. Also, what about weather conditions?
WOMAN: The forecast shows clear skies, but it can get quite chilly in the morning. Wear layers and bring rain gear just in case. We provide snacks and water, but you should pack a lunch.
MAN: Okay, noted. And what's the total cost for the tour?
WOMAN: The tour itself is $195, which includes transportation and guide services. The lens rental would be extra.

**Q3:** What is NOT included in the tour package?
- 1. Transportation
- 2. Guide services
- 3. Lunch
- 4. Water and snacks
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Transportation: The woman states 'We provide transportation from the city center' as part of the tour.
❌ 2. Guide services: The woman mentions that the tour cost 'includes transportation and guide services.'
✅ 3. Lunch: The woman specifically says 'We provide snacks and water, but you should pack a lunch,' indicating lunch is not included.
❌ 4. Water and snacks: The woman clearly states 'We provide snacks and water' as part of what they offer.

**Q4:** What concern does the man express about his equipment?
- 1. His camera is too basic
- 2. His tripod isn't sturdy enough
- 3. His lens might be insufficient
- 4. His gear isn't weather-proof
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. His camera is too basic: The man doesn't express concern about his camera.
❌ 2. His tripod isn't sturdy enough: The man mentions having a basic tripod but doesn't express concerns about it.
✅ 3. His lens might be insufficient: The man asks 'my lens only goes up to 200mm. Will that be enough?' showing concern about his lens's capability.
❌ 4. His gear isn't weather-proof: No concerns about weather-proofing of equipment are mentioned.

**Q5:** What is the total cost of the basic tour?
- 1. $$50
- 2. $$195
- 3. $$245
- 4. $$400
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. $50: This is the cost of renting a 400mm lens for a day, not the tour itself.
✅ 2. $195: The woman explicitly states 'The tour itself is $195, which includes transportation and guide services.'
❌ 3. $245: This would be the total cost of the tour ($195) plus the lens rental ($50), not just the basic tour.
❌ 4. $400: This amount is not mentioned anywhere in the conversation.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi again, I've been thinking about the lens rental.
WOMAN: Yes, would you like to add it to your booking?
MAN: Actually, I'm a bit concerned about using unfamiliar equipment. Could I try the lens beforehand?
WOMAN: Absolutely! We can arrange a quick tutorial the day before. We want you to feel comfortable with the equipment during the tour.
MAN: That would be great. One more thing - what happens if it rains?
WOMAN: We have a flexible rescheduling policy. If the weather is too severe, we'll reschedule for the next available date at no extra cost.
MAN: Perfect! Then I'd like to go ahead with both the tour and lens rental.
WOMAN: Excellent! Let me get that booked for you. Would you prefer to pay now or when you come in for the lens tutorial?
MAN: I'll pay now to secure everything.

**Q6:** Why does the man return to speak with the woman?
- 1. To discuss payment options
- 2. To express concerns about the lens
- 3. To ask about weather conditions
- 4. To change his booking date
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To discuss payment options: Payment options are discussed later, not as the reason for his return.
✅ 2. To express concerns about the lens: The man says 'I've been thinking about the lens rental' and then expresses he's 'concerned about using unfamiliar equipment.'
❌ 3. To ask about weather conditions: Weather conditions were discussed in the first part of the conversation, not when he returns.
❌ 4. To change his booking date: There's no mention of changing the booking date in the conversation.

**Q7:** What solution does the woman offer for the man's concern?
- 1. A discount on the rental
- 2. A different lens option
- 3. A pre-tour tutorial
- 4. A longer rental period
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A discount on the rental: No discount is mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 2. A different lens option: The woman doesn't suggest an alternative lens.
✅ 3. A pre-tour tutorial: When the man expresses concern about using unfamiliar equipment, the woman offers 'a quick tutorial the day before.'
❌ 4. A longer rental period: The rental period is not extended in the conversation.

**Q8:** What decision does the man make at the end?
- 1. To pay for everything immediately
- 2. To wait until the tutorial day
- 3. To only book the tour
- 4. To postpone his decision
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. To pay for everything immediately: The man says 'I'll pay now to secure everything' at the very end of the conversation.
❌ 2. To wait until the tutorial day: The man explicitly chooses not to wait until the tutorial day to pay.
❌ 3. To only book the tour: The man decides to book both 'the tour and lens rental.'
❌ 4. To postpone his decision: The man makes a definite decision rather than postponing it.

## Practice 138: Expired Prescription Refill | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a pharmacy. The woman is a pharmacist, and the man is a customer requesting a prescription refill.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi there, I need to refill my blood pressure medication. I'm running pretty low.
WOMAN: Of course, I can help you with that. Do you have your prescription bottle with you?
MAN: Yes, here it is. I've been taking this for about six months now.
WOMAN: Let me check your records... I see you're due for a refill, but it looks like this prescription has expired. You'll need to contact your doctor for a new one.
MAN: Oh no, really? I had no idea prescriptions could expire.
WOMAN: Yes, most prescriptions expire after one year. Your doctor needs to evaluate your condition periodically to ensure the medication is still appropriate.
MAN: I see. Is there any way to get an emergency supply until I can see my doctor?
WOMAN: We can provide a three-day emergency supply. Would you like me to do that for you?
MAN: Yes, please. That would be really helpful.

**Q1:** What is the main problem the man faces?
- 1. He lost his medication
- 2. His prescription has expired
- 3. He cannot afford the medication
- 4. The pharmacy is out of his medication
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He lost his medication: The conversation doesn't mention the man losing his medication.
✅ 2. His prescription has expired: The pharmacist clearly states "it looks like this prescription has expired. You'll need to contact your doctor for a new one."
❌ 3. He cannot afford the medication: While cost is mentioned later, it's not the main problem he faces initially.
❌ 4. The pharmacy is out of his medication: There's no mention of the pharmacy being out of stock.

**Q2:** What solution does the pharmacy offer?
- 1. A full prescription refill
- 2. A discount on the medication
- 3. A three-day emergency supply
- 4. An extension on the prescription
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A full prescription refill: They can't provide a full refill because the prescription has expired.
❌ 2. A discount on the medication: While a discount is mentioned later, it's not the initial solution to the expired prescription.
✅ 3. A three-day emergency supply: The pharmacist specifically states "We can provide a three-day emergency supply" until he can see his doctor.
❌ 4. An extension on the prescription: The pharmacy doesn't offer to extend the expired prescription.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, I just called my doctor's office about the prescription renewal.
WOMAN: Oh yes, I remember you from earlier. Were you able to reach them?
MAN: Yes, but they said they sent it to a different pharmacy. Can you help me transfer it here?
WOMAN: Of course! Which pharmacy did they send it to?
MAN: The one on Oak Street, I think. That's where I used to go before moving to this neighborhood.
WOMAN: No problem. I'll contact them right away. I just need you to fill out this transfer request form.
MAN: Sure, I can do that. Do you need my insurance information again?
WOMAN: Yes, please include that on the form. Also, make sure to sign and date at the bottom.
MAN: Got it. Here you go. How long will the transfer take?

**Q3:** Why does the man need to transfer his prescription?
- 1. He changed insurance providers
- 2. His doctor sent it to his old pharmacy
- 3. The current pharmacy is too expensive
- 4. He doesn't trust his previous pharmacy
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He changed insurance providers: There's no mention of changing insurance providers.
✅ 2. His doctor sent it to his old pharmacy: The man states "they said they sent it to a different pharmacy" and clarifies it was sent to "The one on Oak Street... where I used to go before moving to this neighborhood."
❌ 3. The current pharmacy is too expensive: Cost isn't mentioned as a reason for the transfer.
❌ 4. He doesn't trust his previous pharmacy: There's no indication of trust issues with the previous pharmacy.

**Q4:** What must the man provide to transfer his prescription?
- 1. Only his signature
- 2. A doctor's note
- 3. His medical history
- 4. A completed form with insurance information
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Only his signature: More than just a signature is required.
❌ 2. A doctor's note: A doctor's note is not mentioned as a requirement.
❌ 3. His medical history: Medical history is not mentioned as a requirement.
✅ 4. A completed form with insurance information: The pharmacist asks him to "fill out this transfer request form" and specifically says "Yes, please include that [insurance information] on the form. Also, make sure to sign and date at the bottom."

**Q5:** What is implied about the man's previous pharmacy visits?
- 1. He recently moved to a new area
- 2. He was dissatisfied with the service
- 3. He had payment issues
- 4. He frequently changed pharmacies
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. He recently moved to a new area: The man mentions the prescription was sent to "where I used to go before moving to this neighborhood," implying he recently relocated.
❌ 2. He was dissatisfied with the service: There's no indication of dissatisfaction with previous service.
❌ 3. He had payment issues: No payment issues at the previous pharmacy are mentioned.
❌ 4. He frequently changed pharmacies: There's no evidence suggesting he frequently changes pharmacies.

### Audio Transcript
WOMAN: Good news! We've received your prescription transfer.
MAN: That's great! How soon can you fill it?
WOMAN: We can have it ready in about 20 minutes. While you wait, I noticed your insurance has a new prescription savings program.
MAN: Really? I hadn't heard about that.
WOMAN: Yes, it could reduce your copay significantly. Would you like me to check if you're eligible?
MAN: That would be fantastic! I've been paying quite a bit for this medication.
WOMAN: Let me look that up... Yes, you qualify! This will bring your copay down from $40 to $15.
MAN: Wow, that's a big difference! Thank you so much for checking.
WOMAN: You're welcome! We'll call you when your prescription is ready.

**Q6:** What additional service does the woman offer?
- 1. Free delivery
- 2. A medication review
- 3. An insurance savings check
- 4. A prescription reminder service
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Free delivery: Delivery service is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 2. A medication review: The pharmacist doesn't offer to review his medications.
✅ 3. An insurance savings check: The pharmacist says "I noticed your insurance has a new prescription savings program" and offers to "check if you're eligible."
❌ 4. A prescription reminder service: No reminder service is mentioned.

**Q7:** How much money will the man save?
- 1. $$15
- 2. $$25
- 3. $$40
- 4. $$55
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. $15: $15 is the new copay amount, not the savings.
✅ 2. $25: The pharmacist states the copay will go down from $40 to $15, which is a savings of $25.
❌ 3. $40: $40 is the original copay amount, not the savings.
❌ 4. $55: This amount is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q8:** What can be inferred about the pharmacy's service?
- 1. They prioritize cost savings
- 2. They are understaffed
- 3. They have slow processing times
- 4. They lack insurance options
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. They prioritize cost savings: The pharmacist proactively checks for insurance savings programs without being asked, showing they prioritize helping customers save money.
❌ 2. They are understaffed: There's no indication of understaffing in the conversation.
❌ 3. They have slow processing times: The pharmacy offers to have the prescription ready in 20 minutes, which seems reasonably quick.
❌ 4. They lack insurance options: The conversation indicates they work with insurance and even know about savings programs.

## Practice 139: Home Charger Installation | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two people at a community meeting. The woman is a resident and the man is an electric vehicle charging station installer.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** What is the woman's main concern about installing a charging station?
- 1. The installation timeline
- 2. The electrical capacity of her house
- 3. The maintenance requirements
- 4. The total cost
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The installation timeline: While she does ask about this, it's not her primary concern.
✅ 2. The electrical capacity of her house: The woman specifically mentions her house is old (from the 1980s) and asks about her electrical panel, showing this is her main concern.
❌ 3. The maintenance requirements: She asks about maintenance, but this appears to be a secondary concern.
❌ 4. The total cost: Although she mentions cost at the beginning, her follow-up questions focus more on the electrical capacity of her older home.

**Q2:** What potential issue might the woman face with her home?
- 1. The age of her electrical panel
- 2. The size of her garage
- 3. The location of her driveway
- 4. The type of foundation
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. The age of her electrical panel: The man specifically mentions that homes from the 1980s typically have 100-amp service, which might need an upgrade depending on other electrical appliances.
❌ 2. The size of her garage: There is no mention of garage size in the conversation.
❌ 3. The location of her driveway: There is no mention of driveway location in the conversation.
❌ 4. The type of foundation: There is no mention of foundation type in the conversation.

**Q3:** How long will the complete installation process take?
- 1. One day
- 2. One week
- 3. Two weeks
- 4. One month
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. One day: The man specifically states that the actual installation takes one day, but this is not the complete process.
❌ 2. One week: This timeframe is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 3. Two weeks: The man clearly states, "the entire process, including permits and inspections, typically takes about two weeks."
❌ 4. One month: This timeframe is not mentioned and is longer than what the man indicates.

**Q4:** What unexpected information does the man share?
- 1. The warranty period
- 2. Available rebates
- 3. Installation timeline
- 4. Maintenance costs
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The warranty period: The warranty information is mentioned but not presented as surprising information.
✅ 2. Available rebates: The man mentions rebates at the end, saying "Many residents don't realize they can get up to 30% of the cost covered," indicating this is unexpected information that most people aren't aware of.
❌ 3. Installation timeline: The timeline is discussed but not presented as surprising information.
❌ 4. Maintenance costs: Maintenance is discussed but no specific costs are mentioned as surprising.

**Q5:** Why is the woman considering this installation?
- 1. To reduce energy costs
- 2. To help the environment
- 3. To increase property value
- 4. To follow neighborhood regulations
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To reduce energy costs: This reason is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 2. To help the environment: While this might be implied, it's not explicitly stated in the conversation.
✅ 3. To increase property value: The woman specifically asks if the charging station will add value to her house since she's planning to sell in a few years, and seems pleased when the man confirms it will be a significant selling point.
❌ 4. To follow neighborhood regulations: There's no mention of any regulations requiring installation.

## Practice 140: Food Drive Volunteering | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two volunteers at a community food drive. The woman is organizing the event and the man is a new volunteer.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** What is the main reason the man decided to volunteer?
- 1. He saw an advertisement at the community center.
- 2. A friend recommended the program.
- 3. He was asked by his business network.
- 4. He regularly volunteers at food drives.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. He saw an advertisement at the community center: The man explicitly states "I saw the flyer at the community center and thought it would be a great way to help out."
❌ 2. A friend recommended the program: There's no mention of a friend's recommendation in the conversation.
❌ 3. He was asked by his business network: He offers to spread the word to his business network, but they didn't prompt him to volunteer.
❌ 4. He regularly volunteers at food drives: He states it's his first time volunteering.

**Q2:** What is the current situation at the food bank?
- 1. They have too many donations.
- 2. They need more storage space.
- 3. They lack specific food items.
- 4. They have enough volunteers.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They have too many donations: The conversation indicates they need more donations, not that they have too many.
❌ 2. They need more storage space: There's no mention of storage space issues in the conversation.
✅ 3. They lack specific food items: The woman specifically mentions they're "especially short on protein items like canned tuna and beans. Also, breakfast cereals... and baby formula."
❌ 4. They have enough volunteers: While this isn't directly addressed, they're actively recruiting more volunteers like the man and for the upcoming supermarket drive.

**Q3:** What will volunteers do at the supermarket collection drive?
- 1. Stock shelves with food items.
- 2. Distribute flyers and collect donations.
- 3. Sort expired food items.
- 4. Transport food to families.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Stock shelves with food items: This task isn't mentioned for the supermarket drive.
✅ 2. Distribute flyers and collect donations: The woman explicitly states "We hand out flyers with suggested items as people enter the store, and then collect donations as they leave."
❌ 3. Sort expired food items: This is what they're doing at the current food drive, not at the supermarket collection.
❌ 4. Transport food to families: There's no mention of transporting food to families during the supermarket drive.

**Q4:** How many families did the food bank help last month?
- 1. More than 200 families
- 2. Exactly 200 families
- 3. Less than 200 families
- 4. This information is not provided
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. More than 200 families: The conversation doesn't support this answer.
❌ 2. Exactly 200 families: The woman mentions they're aiming to support 200 families this month, not that they helped exactly 200 last month.
✅ 3. Less than 200 families: The woman states "Last month, we actually ran out of supplies before we could help everyone," indicating they helped fewer than their target of 200 families.
❌ 4. This information is not provided: The information is indirectly provided when the woman mentions they ran out of supplies before helping everyone.

**Q5:** What is the man's role in the local community?
- 1. A business network member
- 2. A community center worker
- 3. A regular volunteer
- 4. A supermarket employee
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. A business network member: The man mentions "I'm part of a local business network," indicating this is his role in the community.
❌ 2. A community center worker: The conversation doesn't indicate he works at the community center, only that he saw a flyer there.
❌ 3. A regular volunteer: The woman asks if this is his first time volunteering and he confirms "Yes, it is!"
❌ 4. A supermarket employee: There's no mention of him working at a supermarket.

❌ 3. It's aligned with other planets: There's no mention of alignment with other planets.

❌ 2. Once a month: The man says they meet twice a month, not just once.
✅ 3. Twice a month: The man clearly states 'We meet twice a month, usually on clear nights.'
❌ 4. Only during special events: While they do organize special events, their regular meetings occur twice a month.

- 3. Nothing special

**Q5:** What is the typical attendance at club meetings?

- 4. A small group

- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Thirty members: The man says they have about thirty regular members total, not that thirty attend each meeting.
✅ 2. About fifteen members: The man states 'About thirty regular members, but usually fifteen or so show up each meeting.'
